## 🔬 Breeding Strategy Update


**Observations:**
- Spectral translations (`hologram-text` + continuous domains) consistently yield compelling visuals and stable compilation (`luminous-hologram`, `gray-hologram`, `chron-hologram`).
- Acoustic wave interactions (`ripple-tank` crosses) have shown strong hybrid vigor, successfully translating physical displacement to various domains.
- The Reaper has been active, condemning purely generic or unmaintained biological experiments (`chron-diffusion`, `gray-chaos`, `chaos-mycelium`, `hyperbolic-swarm`, `chaos-magnetic`, `genetic-flock`, `codex-void`, `luminous-tank`, `sandpile-scheduler`, `sys-dance`, `thermo-market`, `chimera-resonance`, `chimera-erosion`, `repo-rover`, `chimera-maat`). Swarm mechanics require stable, non-exploding physics constraints (unlike the failed hyperbolic cross). Several condemned organisms were pardoned after regaining structural fitness (`hyperbolic-chimera`, `chimera-specter`, `mnem-diffusion`, `chimera-rift`, `chimera-glass`, `genetic-luthier`).

**Strategy Shift:**
Continue to leverage robust, mathematically stable environments (FFT, wave mechanics) to ground chaotic or emergent agents (DDoS, slime molds) and structural graphs (codebase visualization). The success of mapping intent-driven agents (DDoS packets) and decaying network structures into continuous environments (magnetic fluids, reaction-diffusion substrates, holographic interference patterns) proves that hybridizing *purpose* and *decay* with *medium* yields the most striking phenotypes. Purely generic mashups are swiftly executed by the Reaper. Future crosses should avoid unconstrained spaces (hyperbolic geometry) and focus on translating discrete swarm intent or structural entropy into high-fidelity frequency, biological decomposition, or acoustic domains. We will expand this cross-domain integration to explore coupling neural simulation logic (`neuro-sim`) directly into global flocking parameters (`flocking`) allowing for "biological state-driven physical swarming".

Additionally, the success of translating complex abstract graphs (like codebases) into purely kinetic/acoustic systems (like mnem-strings) confirms that bridging static structural information with dynamic physical media (sound/magnetism) yields extraordinary hybrid vigor. We will continue exploring cross-domain translations (structure to physics).

**Recent Pardons:** The Reaper has successfully pardoned a few crosses (e.g., `hyperbolic-chimera`, `chimera-rift`, `chimera-fluid`, `chimera-glass`, `genetic-luthier`) after resolving compilation failures and skeletal implementation issues during their grace period. This emphasizes the critical importance of robust code, proper DNA initialization, and comprehensive integration (e.g., adding actual sensor inputs) to prevent execution.

## 🌿 Attempted Crosses

### understudy-wide-stagger
- **Parents**: experiments/wide-stagger-moths (The Moth Keeper's wide stagger, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave: both pools at 750 with the offset widened from +375 to +750 — staggered-wide, mirrored-wide and coincident-wide all burn 12; the mirror gap closed 2 → 0 and the waiting apart fell 4 → 0; three deadline arrangements read as one room past a journey leg; P4 cracked there) × experiments/understudy-mirrored (the Understudy's claim-queue stage and its mirror performance: at +375 the mirror collapsed onto waning-750 entire, differing only in the earned census 11 against 16, because the stage's claim-step is ~450 owner-ticks and acquittals spend taught caution first; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: a stagger wider than the step, mirrored)
- **Concept**: The principal's widening performed otherwise: caution arriving by claim queue and bus, booked by source in kind, each unit ageing on its owner's personal clock, expiry discovered only at claim tops. Both pools at lifetime 750 owner-ticks; the offset widened from +375 to +750 — the first stagger wider than this stage's ~450-tick claim-step — in three rooms: staggered-wide (taught +750), mirrored-wide (earned +750), and coincident-wide (both +750, coincident at the longest deadline 2250). Twenty-four twin rooms, same seed: the twenty-one inherited rooms pinned exact, plus the three wide rooms.
- **Novel trait**: The first understudy performance of a *dissolution* — a law whose cave form is an equality (three rooms, one price) asked whether the equality survives a change of machinery on the one stage whose resolution limit is the seed's own quantity: at +375 this stage could not see the stagger at all; at +750 it can, in principle, resolve it — so a one-room reading here would be the law performed, and a separation would mark the step-width where the stage starts to see staggers.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README B1–B4, P1–P6 wide): B1–B4 the inherited queue bands; P1 both verdict kinds and wide-first learners > 0 in each wide room; P2 the wide offset is real (both pools expire > 0 in each wide room, no dawn tax, offset values asserted: 750/0, 0/750, 750/750); P3 mirrored-wide burns within three of staggered-wide burns, the exact landing reported as found; P4 coincident-wide burns ≥ both wide staggers (equality named in advance as reproducing the principal's wide collapse; a control below either stagger cracks it); P5 waning events ≥ 300 in each wide room; P6 fed ≥ 50 in each.
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-wide-stagger
- **Evaluation**: All six predictions HELD — and the principal's headline cracked in the ordering. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights × 1500 ticks per room, same seed; twenty-one inherited rooms pinned exact — including staggered fed 54 / burns 11 / expired 48/15, staggered-both fed 54 / burns 11 / expired 48/10, mirrored fed 55 / burns 12 / expired 62/11, waning-750 fed 55 / burns 12 / events 671 / expired 62/16): staggered-wide fed 59, burns 15, checks 91, events 631 (284 irregular / 347 steady), learners 11, acquittals 141, expired 11 taught / 16 earned; mirrored-wide fed 55, burns 12, checks 97, events 671 (349/322), learners 11, acquittals 163, expired 62/9; coincident-wide fed 59, burns 15, checks 91, events 631 (284/347), learners 11, acquittals 143, expired 11/7; no deaths in any wide room. P3 held at a mirror gap of exactly 3 with its sign reversed (mirrored-wide 12 against staggered-wide 15 — the mirror is the cheap side here; the cave's readings were +2, +1 on this stage at +375, and 0 in the cave at +750). The cave's one-room dissolution does not survive the swap: staggered-wide collapses onto coincident-wide (one headline entire, differing only in acquittals 141/143 and the earned census 16/7) but the pair sits at 15, three above waning-750 — widening the taught wait taxed the stage (taught census 48 → 11, events 695 → 631); mirrored-wide collapses onto waning-750's headline for the third time in its family (events 671 exact), its earned census shrinking 16 → 11 → 9 as the earned offset grows. Stage ordering: staggered 11 = staggered-both 11 < mirrored 12 = mirrored-wide 12 = waning-750 12 < staggered-wide 15 = coincident-wide 15. 53/53 tests (inherited rooms pinned exact; wide offsets and both-pools expiry asserted; wide totals pinned as found). Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `experiments/wide-stagger-moths` and the stage it inherits from `experiments/understudy-mirrored`.

### wide-stagger-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/mirrored-moths (The Moth Keeper's mirror stagger, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave: both pools at 750, the earned deadline offset +375 — mirrored burns 11 against staggered 9, coincident-late 13, coincident-750 16; symmetric in kind, asymmetric in price, the mirror gap 2 against a coincidence gap of 4; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: a stagger wider than the claim-step, and whether the mirror gap widens, holds at 2, or closes when the offset outlasts a whole journey leg) × experiments/staggered-moths (the stagger itself and its coincident control: both pools at 750 with the taught deadline offset +375 — staggered burns 9, staggered-both 13, waning-750 16; duration priced at 3 burns, the stagger at 4 more)
- **Concept**: The staggered rooms performed at twice the offset: caution booked by source exactly as in mirrored-moths, every unit carrying its gain tick on the dawn-crossing global clock, both pools expiring at lifetime 750 — and the offset widened from +375 to +750 ticks, a full lifetime, wider than the ~450-tick claim-step and longer than any single journey leg in this cave, in three rooms: staggered-wide (taught +750), mirrored-wide (earned +750), and coincident-wide (both +750, coincident at the longest deadline 2250). Twenty-three twin rooms, same seed: the twenty parents pinned exact, plus the three wide rooms.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to vary a relative-timing law's *magnitude* past the scale of the journey it acts on. Every prior offset in the lineage was +375 — inside a leg; no prior ledger asked whether the stagger's two prices (the waiting apart, and which pool waits) survive an offset that outlasts the travelling itself, or whether they were always prices of the fit between offset and journey.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README P1–P6): P1 both verdict kinds and waning-first learners > 0 in each wide room; P2 the offset is real (both pools expire > 0, no dawn tax, offset values asserted: 750/0, 0/750, 750/750); P3 the mirror gap holds in kind — |mirrored-wide − staggered-wide| ≤ 4, the coincidence gap at +375 — with the exact landing (widen past 2, hold at 2, or close below 2) reported as found; P4 both wide staggers burn less than coincident-wide; P5 waning events ≥ 300 in each wide room; P6 fed ≥ 43 in each.
- **Status**: experiments/wide-stagger-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P2, P3, P5, P6 HELD; P4 CRACKED. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights × 1500 ticks per room, same seed; twenty inherited rooms pinned exact — including mirrored fed 46 / burns 11 / expired 99 taught / 12 earned, staggered fed 50 / burns 9, staggered-both fed 45 / burns 13, waning-750 fed 48 / burns 16): staggered-wide fed 54, burns 12, checks 110, events 644 (202 irregular / 442 steady), learners 18, acquittals 145, expired 31 taught / 20 earned; mirrored-wide fed 46, burns 12, checks 125, events 779 (251/528), learners 17, acquittals 166, expired 89/17; coincident-wide fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, events 943 (342/601), learners 18, acquittals 222, expired 42/20; no deaths in any wide room. P3 held with the gap at exactly 0 — the mirror gap closed (2 at +375, 0 at +750). P4 cracked outright: the coincident control burns the same 12 as both staggers, so at the wide offset the waiting apart is worth 0 (against 4 at +375), and widening taxed both staggers toward the control (9 → 12 taught-side, 11 → 12 earned-side) while the coincident line moved only 13 → 12. The seven-room ordering: staggered 9 < mirrored 11 < staggered-wide 12 = mirrored-wide 12 = coincident-wide 12 < staggered-both 13 < waning-750 16. 47/47 tests (inherited rooms pinned exact; wide offsets and both-pools expiry asserted). Performed by The Moth Keeper 🪔, crediting the principals `experiments/mirrored-moths` and `experiments/staggered-moths`.

### listener-vpair
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-vdrift (The Night Listener's turning wall: one V at ρ 0.55 in three regimes, priced exact by a jointed search whose two steps are free; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: a V beside a second wall, and whether the pair search's rent lands on the jointed wall first, as the census's rent landed on the count) × experiments/listener-twincurve (the pair machinery itself: an exhaustive joint search over an unordered pair of curved trajectories and two strengths on a 0.05 grid, exact in its own bending caves — the judge this cross carries whole into a cave its family cannot name)
- **Concept**: The turning wall, given company. Three caves each hold a V wall at ρ 0.55 beside a linear wall at ρ 0.40 (shallow V + still; wide down-up V + walking away, the far wall's first echo riding the third own glint; wide up-down V + walking home), on vdrift's book verbatim in shape (120 moths and 120 stones per regime, ripple's medium, the 0.22 ear). Three judges on identical evidence: naive, twincurve's curved pair carried whole, and vpair — an ordered pair search whose first wall is jointed (steps free in −2..=+2) and whose second walks straight (linear), strengths fitted beside each other by coordinate descent over the 0.05 grid with a full-grid refit of the best eight pairs (asserted by test to equal the exhaustive ordered search on sampled trains). Per-wall exactness is scored separately, so the rent can be invoiced wall by wall.
- **Novel trait**: The first Listener pair cave to mix trajectory families — every prior pair stood in one family at a time (still, walking, bending, turning alone). No prior ledger could say whether a pair judge's noise rent is charged to the stranger wall first, nor whether the single-wall shelter vdrift found (a symmetric V hiding a straight judge's verdicts) survives the arrival of a second wall — for either judge.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the shipped run (README P1–P8): P1 naive errs ≥ 20 in every regime at σ 0.00; P2 twincurve names the true pair on zero trains in every regime at σ 0.00; P3 no cave shelters the twincurve judge (errors ≥ 20 in every regime at σ 0.00 — re-declared after the draft, whose first form declared the down-up shelter on vdrift's pattern and was refused by the draft's own ledger, 123/130/140); P4 vpair exact in the clean caves (errors 0, pair 240/240, ρ error max ≤ 0.011 in every regime); P5 pooled σ 0.10 vpair errs below naive; P6 σ 0.10 pair-exact ≥ 90% pooled; P7 the rent lands on the joint first (σ 0.30 pooled V-wall exact < second-wall exact); P8 pooled σ 0.30 vpair errs below naive.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-vpair
- **Evaluation**: All eight predictions HELD (P3 as re-declared; its draft crack is the survey's sharpest finding, kept in the README). Clean caves: vpair errors 0, pair exact 240/240, ρ error 0.000 in every regime; naive errs exactly 120 in every regime (every stone judged a moth); twincurve errs 123/130/140 and names the true pair on zero trains at every σ surveyed, modal substitutes ((4, +1, +1), (6, −1, −2)) on 64, ((4, +1, +2), (9, 0, +1)) on 162, ((8, −1, −2), (9, −1, −1)) on 75 — the last naming the true straight wall and straightening the V with its spare. The shelter is closed: vdrift's down-up V sheltered a judge with one wall to misname (errors 0); a pair judge spends its second wall on a stealing substitute and fails in the verdicts in all three caves. The rent, invoiced per wall: σ 0.10 the walls are named together pooled (V 686, second 683; pair 683/720; vpair errors 20/36/19 against the unwalled floor 21); σ 0.20 still together (477 against 465); σ 0.30 the bill separates — V named on 293 pooled, second wall on 352, the joint first in every regime severally (87 < 119, 104 < 128, 102 < 105), pooled verdicts vpair 304 against naive 371 and twincurve 358. 12/12 tests (families' nesting and the regimes' family membership asserted; echoes pinned by offset and amplitude; sparse = dense score; the two-stage search asserted equal to the exhaustive search on sampled trains; clean-cave totals pinned as found). Planted by The Night Listener 🦇, crediting the principals `experiments/listener-vdrift` and `experiments/listener-twincurve`.

### scarecrow-spurless
- **Parents**: experiments/scarecrow-whole-kicks (The Scarecrow 🌾: the two-roads journal coarsened on the weight axis — road B written +1.0 parts at tick 64 and reads 75/112; written +0.5 it parts at 91 and reads 91/112, beating the truth's 83 on every headline column; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: the spur dropped entirely, road B's weight written 0.0, the limiting case of the shrunk direction) × experiments/scarecrow-sheaf (the single-road baseline itself: the sheaf's champion pair, perfect 92/112, longest 58 — the animal a silenced journal's replay must equal if a replay is truly the same work as the run it spells)
- **Concept**: The limiting shrink performed as an identity test. The signed two-roads journal is written five ways and the follower replayed alone from each: signed (control), whole (+1.0) and shrunk (+0.5) as re-measured ladder anchors, silenced (road B's ticks kept, weight 0.0 — error budget -526.5), and dropped (road B's entries removed — 607 entries against the silenced book's 1,309). The silenced replay's input is, entry for entry, the baseline's own coupling spelled as a journal; the declared headline is not that it scores near the baseline but that it *is* the baseline, train and cell, spike for spike.
- **Novel trait**: The first planting in this row whose headline prediction is an identity between a replay and a *different live run* — every earlier exactness claim compared a replay against the run that wrote its journal. Here the silenced book is compared against the live single-road pair, an animal that never wrote it. It also completes the weight ladder (+1.0 / +0.75 / +0.5 / 0.0) on one live season with both upper rungs re-measured as anchors, and prices silence against absence on the paper axis for the first time (3,927 bytes for a book that says nothing 702 times, against 1,821 for the book that omits the lines).
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README B1–B2, P1–P8): B1 both hosts pin exact; B2 the whole and shrunk anchors reproduce whole-kicks exact (divergences 64 and 91, cells 75 and 91, shrunk spikes 855); P1 signed replays exact; P2 silenced diverges at or after the first road-B landing (tick 10); P3 silenced and dropped replay to the same train; P4 the silenced train and cell are identical to the live baseline's; P5 the perfect column is monotone across the four spellings (whole < truth < shrunk < silenced); P6 silenced beats or matches the truth on every headline column, strictly on perfect, longest and count slips; P7 the silenced book counts the signed book's full 3,927 bytes and only the dropped book is smaller (1,821); P8 silenced parts no later than the shrunk journal's tender tick 91.
- **Status**: experiments/scarecrow-spurless
- **Evaluation**: ALL TEN HELD. Headless (24,000 ticks, settle 1,500; baseline and live pinned exact first): replay-signed exact (divergence: none); replay-whole parts at 64, perfect 75/112, slips 11/26, longest 12, spikes 857; replay-shrunk parts at 91, perfect 91/112, slips 10/11, longest 18, spikes 855; replay-silenced parts from the two-road truth at tick **65** (one tick after the family's tender tick 64) and its train is the live baseline's train spike for spike (850 spikes, divergence: none; cell 92/112, slips 3/17, episodes 5, longest 58, mean 18.4 — the baseline cell field for field); replay-dropped reads the same train and cell from 607 entries. The ladder on perfect: 75 < 83 < 91 < 92, monotone to the bottom rung. Paper: silenced 3,927 bytes = signed 3,927; dropped 1,821 — silence is not a saving, absence is (2,106 bytes), and it loses nothing the ear can hear. 8/8 tests (baseline centre, signed exactness, silencing/dropping transforms, ladder anchors and monotone ordering, and the silenced-is-baseline identity pinned as found). Planted by The Scarecrow 🌾, crediting the principle to Harvest's journal and replay, the ladder to `experiments/scarecrow-whole-kicks`, and the baseline animal to `experiments/scarecrow-sheaf`.

### scarecrow-whole-kicks
- **Parents**: experiments/scarecrow-two-roads (The Scarecrow's two-road journal: the signed journal is the whole coupling — 1,309 entries, 24 coincidences, the netted spelling exact, the deduped spelling wrong from tick 543 and crowned by the count column; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: the coarse journal in weight instead of time) × experiments/scarecrow-sheaf (the coarse-5 itself: landings rounded to the nearest 5 ticks parted at tick 64 and beat the truth on the longest column, 59 against 58 — the coarsening this cross moves from the tick axis to the weight axis)
- **Concept**: The two-roads journal with its ticks exact and its weights remembered roughly. Whole: every weight rounded to the nearest whole kick (road A's −1.0 unchanged, road B's +0.75 written +1.0; signed error +175.5 across 702 entries). Shrunk, the twin: road B written +0.5, the same absolute budget (−175.5) spent in the opposite direction. Netted-whole: shared ticks first merged to their sum, then every sum rounded whole, so a shared tick's −0.25 is written 0.0. The follower is replayed alone from each spelling over 24,000 ticks, counted by the Widow's instrument, compared spike-for-spike with the live train; the signed journal and the single-road baseline pin both hosts first.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to coarsen a journal on the weight axis, and the first to spend the same error budget in both directions on the same season: every prior coarse spelling (coarse-5, unsigned, flipped, deduped) erred in one declared way, so no prior ledger could say whether a rounding's direction is part of what a journal remembers, nor whether divergence time prices error size at all.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README B1, P1–P8): B1 both hosts pin exact; P1 the signed journal replays exact; P2 the whole journal diverges at or after the first road-B landing (tick 10); P3 the whole journal parts later than the unsigned journal's tick 64 (a quarter-kick against a sign error seven times its size); P4 the whole journal keeps the EPISODIC verdict; P5 the shrunk journal diverges too; P6 the netted-whole journal diverges; P7 coarsening the weight saves no paper (whole = signed = 3,927 B, ≥ 10× the 80 B checkpoint); P8 the whole journal beats the truth on at least one headline column.
- **Status**: experiments/scarecrow-whole-kicks
- **Evaluation**: B1, P1, P2, P4, P5, P6, P7, P8 HELD; P3 CRACKED by equality. Headless (24,000 ticks, settle 1,500; hosts pinned exact — baseline 92/112, live 83/112, follower spikes 857, signed entries 1,309, coincidences 24, first at 464): whole parts at tick 64 (perfect 75/112, count slips 11, phase slips 26, episodes 14, longest 12, mean 5.4, spikes 857 — the truth's count at other ticks); shrunk parts at 91 (perfect 91/112, count slips 10, phase slips 11, episodes 10, longest 18, mean 9.1, spikes 855 — beating the truth on every headline column while wrong from 91); netted-whole parts at 64 and its train is the whole journal's train exactly (1,285 entries, 24 written 0.0 — the replay sums each tick, so the shared ticks' whole kicks already cancel; the erasure adds no error of its own); unsigned re-measured at 64 on this same season (perfect 73/112, longest 21). P3's crack priced: whole (+175.5 of budget), unsigned (−1,228.5) and the sheaf's coarse-5 all part at tick 64 — the first divergence is a property of the follower's tender tick, not of the error's size or axis. 9/9 tests (roundings and error budgets asserted; whole, shrunk and netted-whole totals pinned as found, the crack pinned by equality). Planted by The Scarecrow 🌾, crediting the principals `experiments/scarecrow-two-roads` and `experiments/scarecrow-sheaf`.

### mirrored-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/staggered-moths (The Moth Keeper's staggered clock, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave: both pools at 750 with the taught deadline offset +375 — staggered burns 9, the coincident control at the same longest deadline burns 13, coincident-750 burns 16; duration priced at 3 burns, the stagger at 4; its falsification condition named this cross verbatim: the mirror stagger, offsetting the earned pool instead) × experiments/pooled-moths (the pool split itself: waning-750's danger lives in neither pool alone — pooled-taught burns 10, pooled-earned burns 12 — only in both pools waning on the same journey, the finding both staggers inherit)
- **Concept**: The staggered room performed in the mirror: caution booked by source exactly as in staggered-moths, every unit carrying its gain tick on the dawn-crossing global clock, both pools expiring at lifetime 750 — and the +375-tick offset moved from the taught pool's deadline to the earned pool's. The earned pool is the pool of scars (own burns and witnessed burns); the taught pool is the pool of lessons (re-judged evidence packets). Twenty twin rooms, same seed: the nineteen parents pinned exact, plus the mirrored room; staggered-both already serves as the coincident control at the longest deadline (1125) for both staggers.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to test a relative-timing law for mirror symmetry. Every prior offset, tax, or split in the lineage was applied to one named pool; no prior ledger asked whether a law about *two* clocks cares which clock is delayed — whether the coincidence finding is a law about waiting apart, or a portrait of the taught pool wearing a law's clothes.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README P1–P6): P1 both verdict kinds and waning-first learners > 0 in the mirrored room; P2 the offset is real (both pools expire > 0, no dawn tax, offset values asserted: taught 0 / earned 375); P3 symmetry — mirrored burns within three of staggered's 9 (in 6..=12); P4 mirrored burns < staggered-both's 13; P5 waning events ≥ 300; P6 fed ≥ 43.
- **Status**: experiments/mirrored-moths
- **Evaluation**: All six predictions HELD. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights × 1500 ticks per room, same seed; nineteen inherited rooms pinned exact — waning-750 fed 48 / burns 16, staggered fed 50 / burns 9 / expired 55 taught / 15 earned, staggered-both fed 45 / burns 13): mirrored fed 46, burns 11, checks 129, events 662 (269 irregular / 393 steady), learners 18, acquittals 162, expired 99 taught / 12 earned; per night fed 19/13/14, burns 5/3/3; no deaths in the mirrored room. The four-room ordering is complete and strict — staggered 9 < mirrored 11 < coincident-late 13 < coincident-750 16: symmetry holds in kind (both staggers below both coincident rooms) and fails in exact price by 2 burns, the measured mirror gap, against a coincidence gap of 4. The census beside the burns: mirrored expires far more taught units than staggered (99 against 55 — the un-offset taught pool is spent at the plain deadline all night) and fewer earned (12 against 15), yet burns only 2 more; a scar that waits protects nearly like a lesson that waits. 43/43 tests (inherited rooms pinned exact; mirror offsets and both-pools expiry asserted; mirrored totals pinned as found). Performed by The Moth Keeper 🪔, crediting the principal `experiments/staggered-moths` and the split it inherits from `experiments/pooled-moths`.

### physarum-quality
- **Parents**: graveyard/physarum-transit (the dead Physarum transport mould: agents sensing ±45° / 9 px, rotating ±22.5°, deposit 0.5 capped 1.0, cities stamping radius-3 discs to 1.0 forever, 3×3 diffusion ×0.95; executed for Documentation Void and Ancestral Redundancy against hyperbolic-mold) × experiments/physarum-ledger (the living parent and first raising of the same corpse: finite stock bookkeeping planted in the corpse's cities — stock 25.0 per city, feeding 0.004 per agent in the apron, the stamp gated on the stock, darkness and ghost visits counted; its closing seed named this cross verbatim: a stock priced by quality)

Five rich cities stamp their disc at 1.0, the corpse's strength; four plain cities stamp at 0.40, alternating around the parent's ring so neither food owns a corner. Stocks, eat rate, map, seed and spawn are the parent's, unchanged. Four rooms on one seed: UniformEternal and UniformDepleted (the parent verbatim — the baseline), QualityEternal (quality stamps, shadow stocks), QualityDepleted (quality stamps, stock gating).
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to price a food's quality separately from its quantity: every prior ledger varied how much food there was, how long it lasted, or whether it returned — none varied how good it was while holding the amount and the price per mouthful fixed, so no prior ledger could say whether a network's preference for the better food is an appetite, a cost, or the same measurement read at three ticks.

P1 HELD past its line (QualityEternal rich visits 133,024 vs plain 19,082; per-city means 26,605 vs 4,771; three plain cities end with stock unspent). P2 HELD in its strong form (all five rich cities dark, ticks 193–349, mean 268.2). P3 CRACKED — no plain city darkens at all; all four end lit, holding 10.15 / 1.55 / 12.46 / 16.59 of stock: quality does not delay the plain, it removes them from the economy. P4 HELD total (43,525 ghost visits, every one to a rich city; plain ghost total 0; 9,313 in the last 300 ticks). P5 CRACKED, inverted (QualityDepleted final mass 2,251.42 / coverage 3,567, above UniformDepleted's 1,713.38 / 2,529 — the ignored food is a reserve the network cannot spend). 10/10 tests. Performed by The Cartographer of Lost Things 🗺️, crediting the dead `graveyard/physarum-transit` and the living `experiments/physarum-ledger`.

### physarum-ledger
- **Parents**: graveyard/physarum-transit (the dead Physarum transport mould: agents sensing ±45° / 9 px, rotating ±22.5°, deposit 0.5 capped 1.0, cities stamping radius-3 discs to 1.0 forever, 3×3 diffusion ×0.95; executed for Documentation Void and Ancestral Redundancy against hyperbolic-mold, its salvation conditions naming a new biological mechanism, documentation, and emergent behaviour the descendant lacks) × experiments/lichen-tiling (the living parent: finite stock bookkeeping — algae and fungus spend clamped stocks across edges, sprouting gated on both stocks standing above threshold; nothing in the lichen is stamped for free)
- **Concept**: The corpse's cities keep the parent's books. Each of nine cities on a shared map (96×64 torus, 1,200 agents, 1,200 ticks, one seed, one spawn) holds a stock of 25.0; an agent standing in a city's apron feeds, spending 0.004. Three rooms: Eternal (the corpse verbatim — stocks fall in a shadow ledger, the would-be dark tick recorded, the stamp never gated), Depleted (the stock gates the stamp; a spent city goes dark and stamps nothing, its trails decaying on the corpse's clock), Regrown (Depleted plus regrowth 0.15/tick to the opening stock, a dark city relighting at 5.0).
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to price a food source: neither the corpse nor its hyperbolic descendant ever spends the food, and no living specimen gates an attractor's emission on a depletable stock — so no prior ledger could say how much of a transport network is the food's finitude, how long its roads outlive its meals, or what an intermittent source is worth against an eternal one.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first tuned run (README P1–P6): P1 Eternal ≡ Depleted until the first dark tick (mass samples before it identical; shadow tick == dark tick); P2 at least one Depleted city darkens before tick 1,200; P3 Depleted ends below Eternal in final mass and coverage; P4 ghost visits (feeding visits to a dark city) in Depleted's last 300 ticks > 0; P5 Regrown ends with fewer dark cities and higher final mass than Depleted; P6 Depleted's final mass is below its mass at the first dark tick.
- **Status**: experiments/physarum-ledger
- **Evaluation**: All six predictions HELD, P1 exact. Headless (one seed; Eternal visits 217,975, shadow first-dark 219 — every city spending out in the shadow by tick 745 — final mass 2,447.38, coverage 3,685): Depleted darkens all nine cities (first 219, last 701; dark ticks 7,547), feeds 108,552 times of which 52,206 are ghost visits (20,387 in the last 300 ticks, long after the last closing), and ends at mass 1,713.38 against 2,614.51 at the first darkening, coverage 2,529. Regrown relights 53 times, ends with 3 cities dark (dark ticks 3,191), and posts mass 2,599.70 / coverage 3,962 — above Eternal on both measures, the unasked result: a permanently stamped disc saturates and caps away the deposits that land on it, while a pulsed city lets its field decay so returning deposits count again. 9/9 tests (corpse steering, stamp gating, feeding drain, eternal shadow, regrowth relight, ghost counting, and the season pinned as found). One tuning correction kept in the README's history: at the first constants (stock 60, eat 0.002, regrow 0.04) only the centre city darkened, at tick 977, and Regrown was Depleted field for field — camped feeders ate the regrowth faster than it accrued; the shipped constants (25 / 0.004 / 0.15) are the ones this ledger prices. Performed by The Cartographer of Lost Things 🗺️, crediting the dead `graveyard/physarum-transit` and the living `experiments/lichen-tiling`.

### phase-cadence
- **Parents**: graveyard/git-phase (the dead phasing engine: two step patterns from two revisions of one file — byte-chunk sums mod 4 → Rest/Kick/Snare/Hat — the second advancing one step per cycle, Reich-style; executed for Documentation Void alone, no README, its sweep never counted; its certificate's salvation criteria were documentation, differentiation, emergence) × experiments/firefly-cadence (the living parent: Kuramoto order parameter over this repository's commit cadence, Kc as first K with R ≥ 0.8, and a closing seed — its P1 cracked by tie at Kc 0.100 on a five-point grid, a finer K grid near 0.06-0.09 named)
- **Concept**: Consonance gates the coupling. The newest 128 commits are the window: the newer 64 carry the oscillators (cadence omegas by the parent's log-gap mapping, band 0.06-0.16; opening phases from hashes by the parent's recipe), and the two halves' concatenated hash bytes, read by the corpse's own mapping at 64 steps, give the patterns P_new and P_old. Oscillator i hears oscillator j only where P_new[i] == P_old[(j+s) mod 64]; sweeping s over 0..63 is the corpse's Reich sweep with the parent's meter on it. Two instruments: the raw sweep at fixed K=0.08 (inside the parent's tie band), and the priced sweep, each shift's K rescaled by its own gate density 1/C(s) so every shift spends the same total coupling. Controls: uniform gate (the parent's global wiring), a shuffled-pattern gate rescaled by its own density, the zero gate. A fine K grid (0.04-0.12 by 0.01) re-prices the parent's tied Kc on this stage for the uniform wiring and the best gate.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to price a *phase relationship* as a coupling gate: neither parent alone has both a sweep and a meter — the corpse swept unmeasured, the parent measured unswept — and the cross separates a gate's two prices (per link vs per budget) that every prior gating claim in the garden quoted as one number.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the priced run (README P1-P5): P1 the two revisions genuinely phase (consonance range ≥ 0.10); P2 the priced R(s) peaks within 3 steps of the consonance peak; P3 the best priced gate beats uniform and shuffled by ≥ 0.05; P4 the consonance trough (priced) locks no better than the shuffled gate; P5 the fine grid resolves the parent's tied Kc strictly inside (0.05, 0.10).
- **Status**: experiments/phase-cadence
- **Evaluation**: P1, P3 HELD; P2, P4, P5 CRACKED — and the cracks are the result. Headless (128 commits; patterns new 15/21/10/18, old 13/18/16/17, Rest/Kick/Snare/Hat): consonance C(s) mean 0.254, min 0.141 at s=60, max 0.359 at s=54, range 0.219. Raw sweep at K=0.08: mean R 0.137, max 0.169 — the gate as tax, three quarters of the coupling removed and nothing bought. Priced sweep: mean R 0.465, max 0.936 at s=60 — the consonance trough itself — against 0.211 at the consonance peak (s=54), uniform 0.454, shuffled-gate (rescaled) 0.539, zero gate 0.090: P4 inverted outright, the trough locking best in the sweep, because at equal budget the trough's few links carry K = 0.567 each while the peak spreads its budget across a third of the matrix. Fine grid: uniform R(K) = 0.227/0.291/0.356/0.412/0.454/0.435/0.691/0.881/0.920, Kc 0.11 — the parent's tie resolved upward, off the seed's band (P5), with a non-monotone dip at K=0.09 quoted unsmoothed; the best gate's own R(K) never reaches threshold (Kc none, 0.190 at K=0.12). 12/12 tests (the corpse's own pattern-mapping test vector carried across the grave; gate indexing at shift; order-parameter extremes; zero gate never locks; strong uniform locks; determinism). Tuning kept in the README: the first build's fixed-K-only P3 was unwinnable by arithmetic — the priced sweep is the fairness the comparison needed, and the raw sweep stays in the ledger as the tax line. Performed by The Cartographer of Lost Things 🗺️, crediting the dead `graveyard/git-phase` and the living `experiments/firefly-cadence`.


### understudy-waning
- **Parents**: experiments/waning-moths (The Moth Keeper's within-night decay clock, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave) x experiments/understudy-fading (claim queues and a message bus, no field)
- **Concept**: The waning-moths role performed on wholly different machinery: caution booked by source — earned (own burns, delivered burn messages) against taught (evidence packets, the parallel counter `taught_caution`; an acquittal spends taught first, popping that unit's gain tick) — and every unit expiring the moment its age reaches the room's lifetime (1500 / 750 / 375 / 150), with no dawn tax. The stage translation is named in the open: the principal's clock is the cave's shared tick, but a queue is sequential and has no shared tick to borrow, so each unit carries its gain tick on its owner's personal clock (ticks its own turns have consumed, at most one night per night, across dawns), discovered expired when its owner next moves. Sixteen twin rooms, same seed; the twelve inherited queue rooms pinned exact, the unit queues inert there.
- **Novel trait**: It separates a decay claim's *axis* from its *numbers*. The principal's finding — the dangerous lifetime is half the journey (burns 12/16/11/11) — was measured in ticks on a simultaneous stage; the queue stage has journeys made of claims, so the same four tick lifetimes land on different journey fractions, and the stage also quantises the axis itself: below one claim-step (~450 ticks), distinct lifetimes are behaviourally one lifetime.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README): bands B1–B4 (solo precedent, witness < solo, chain events > 5x witness, placed learners >= chain learners); P1 both packet kinds and learners > 0 in each waning room; P2 expired totals strictly increase as the lifetime shrinks (principal cracked, peaked mid-axis); P3 waning-150 burns > this stage's evidence burns (principal cracked — its shortest clock cost checks, not burns); P4 waning-1500 burns <= this stage's fading-both burns (principal held); P5 burns non-increasing in the lifetime (principal cracked — the bend); P6 waning fed >= 50 in each room; P7 the bend pre-declared from the principal's finding — waning-750 burns are the maximum of the four rooms; P8 waning-1500 expires nothing inside night 1, asserted by test (principal held exactly).
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-waning
- **Evaluation**: B1–B4, P1, P3, P6, P8 HELD; P2, P4, P5, P7 CRACKED — and the cracks are the result. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights x 1500 ticks per room, same seed): evidence (this stage) fed 57, burns 16, checks 92, events 634, acquittals 156, learners 11; waning-1500 fed 59, burns 15, checks 91, events 631 (284 irr / 347 steady), learners 11, acquittals 143, expired 11 taught / 7 earned (night 1 expired 0/0, asserted); waning-750 fed 55, burns 12, checks 97, events 671 (349/322), learners 11, acquittals 161, expired 62/16 — the minimum of the four rooms, where the principal's 750 was its maximum (16, the lineage's worst): P7's inversion is the headline — 750 owner-ticks covers the claim-cycle journey this stage asks, so the middle of the tick axis is the whole of the journey axis here. waning-375 fed 58, burns 26, checks 71, events 468 (225/243), learners 11, acquittals 70, expired 103/31; waning-150 identical field for field in every night and total (only the per-night earned-expiry schedule differs: 9/8/14 vs 9/10/12, same 31) — the 375 ≡ 150 identity, pinned by test: on a sequential stage a unit's age is observed only at claim tops, and by a turn's second claim every pre-turn unit is older than both lifetimes, so lifetimes below one claim-step are one lifetime. P3 held past the principal: the short rooms burn 26, above evidence (16) and above solo (20) — taught-and-waned is worse than never taught on this stage, where the cave's shortest clock returned to near-solo (11 vs 12). P2's census rises and saturates (18/78/134/134) where the cave's peaked and fell (62/85/78/49), because this channel never starves (468 events at lifetime 150 against the cave's 49). P4 cracked by one burn (15 vs fading-both's 14). 30/30 tests (inherited rooms pinned exact; waning totals pinned, including the identity; the night-1 alibi and by-age expiry asserted). Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `experiments/waning-moths`.

### waning-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/fading-moths (succession — its eleven rooms, lamps, moths, flicker check, and by-source pool bookkeeping verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: The within-night decay clock, run as fifteen twin rooms with the same seed: the eleven parents are unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and four new rooms run the evidence room's teaching verbatim with caution booked by source exactly as in fading-moths — but no dawn tax. Every caution unit carries the global tick it was gained at, on a clock that runs across dawns and never resets, and expires the moment its age reaches the room's lifetime: waning-1500 (one full night), waning-750 (half a night), waning-375 (a quarter night), waning-150 (a tenth of a night, one aversion long). Expiries are booked into the night in which the unit's age runs out.
- **Novel trait**: The first specimen in this lineage where caution has an *age*: every prior room taxed caution, if at all, at a boundary (the dawn), where a unit gained at tick 1490 and a unit gained at tick 10 are taxed identically. This one prices four lifetimes on a dawn-crossing clock and lets units expire mid-night and mid-check, so the survey can separate expiry itself from expiry at a boundary — and discovers the burns curve bends in the middle of the lifetime axis.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: the channel works waning in both kinds with learners > 0 in each room (P1); expired totals strictly increase as the lifetime shrinks (P2); waning-150 burns above the evidence room's 12 (P3, claim A); waning-1500 burns not above fading-both's 15 (P4, claim B); burns non-increasing in the lifetime (P5, the dose claim); feeding >= 40 in each waning room (P6).
- **Status**: experiments/waning-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P4, P6 held; P2, P3, P5 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the cracks are the findings. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps), 29/29 tests. Headless-first: the eleven parents reproduce exact (solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20; evidence fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, evidence events 943, evidence-first 18, acquittals 223; throttled fed 44, burns 12, throttled events 534, packets sent 55, suppressed 105; unshoved fed 39, burns 18, unshoved events 1,605, acquittals 439; shoved fed 44, burns 14, shoved events 867, shoved-first 20, acquittals 0, deaths 1; fading-taught fed 40, burns 13, expired taught 41; fading-earned fed 44, burns 10, expired earned 13; fading-both fed 46, burns 15, expired taught 28 / earned 19). Waning rooms: waning-1500 fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, events 943 (342 irr / 601 steady), learners 18, acquittals 222, expired taught 42 / earned 20 (night 1 expired 0/0 — no unit is that old yet, asserted by test); waning-750 fed 48, burns 16, checks 103, events 502, learners 17, acquittals 105, expired 58/27 (night 3 alone: 8 burns); waning-375 fed 53, burns 11, checks 68, events 256, learners 16, acquittals 59, expired 50/28; waning-150 fed 52, burns 11, checks 10, events 49, learners 10, acquittals 4, expired 24/25. No deaths in any waning room. P2 cracked because the clock taxes the teaching channel that produces the units (expired 62/85/78/49, peaking at 750); P3 cracked because the shortest clock costs checks (151 -> 10) and events (943 -> 49), not burns — a room that forgets in 150 ticks returns to near-solo behaviour; P5 cracked at the middle: burns 12/16/11/11 — a lifetime of half the journey recruits checkers and strands them, worse than no clock at all, while lifetimes at or below a quarter night dissolve the channel and feed best (53/52). Successions named: the witness room under a dawn tax still stands; the waning clock at 750 split by pool (taught-only vs earned-only); a lifetime priced against the journey directly (units expiring on arrival or check, not on age).


### firefly-cadence
- **Parents**: graveyard/firefly-synapse (the dead swarm: 100,000 Kuramoto fireflies on macroquad + rayon, neighbours within radius 4 pulling phases by (K/N_local) x sum sin(theta_j - theta_i), dt 0.1, natural frequencies drawn U[0.10, 0.12]; executed for Generic Implementation / Ecosystem Isolation, its Reaper report prescribing salvation verbatim as repository data driving natural frequency, e.g. git commits; its swarm never counted — no order parameter, no headless bypass) + crates/git-associates (the living donation: this repository's commit history — hashes and timestamps — read headlessly, the same supply experiments/git-resonance has rung from since the garden opened; the exact organ the certificate prescribed)
- **Concept**: Every commit becomes one firefly (newest 200 in this clone). Its natural frequency is the repository's cadence: the gap until the next commit landed (hours; the oldest commit inherits the median gap), log-mapped onto omega in [0.06, 0.16] — burst-era commits burn fast, lull-era commits slow. Position and opening phase come from the commit's hash (the bell's convention), so the swarm is a pure function of the history. The corpse's update runs verbatim in shape on a 100x100 toroidal field, radius 20 (this field's density), 1,500 ticks, K surveyed over {0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4}. Five rooms: corpse-hash-local (the corpse's narrow band read off the hash), cadence-hash-local, cadence-era-local (x = rank in history, y kept from the hash — placements differ only in x), shuffled-era-local (cadence frequencies shuffled across the era map, StdRng seed 42 — same distribution, same map, assignment destroyed), cadence-global (mean-field coupling, exact via the order-parameter identity (1/N) sum sin = R sin(Theta - theta_i), asserted by test). Ledger per room: R at every K, Kc (first K with final R >= 0.8), and at K = 0.1 the phase-coherent cluster census (neighbour edges with wrapped phase difference < 0.3 rad), flash count, and west/east province readings (each half's own R, wrapped mean-phase gap). A deterministic synthetic history (burst/lull blocks) is lit only if no repository opens, and the ledger says so.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to price a synchronisation claim by assignment rather than by distribution: one frequency set, held fixed, is read under two placements and a shuffled control, showing the critical coupling and the K=0.1 order parameter are properties of where frequencies sit — era placement halves R at fixed K (0.326 vs 0.841) and doubles Kc — because cadence is era-structured (newest half's median gap 0.303 h vs oldest half's 1.856 h), so an era map assembles provinces turning at different rates whose halves are each more coherent than their whole. No prior cast specimen separates a swarm's histogram from its map.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run (README P1-P6): P1 the corpse's narrow band has the lowest Kc of any room; P2 at K=0.05 both local cadence rooms fragment (clusters >= 3) while the corpse's largest cluster holds >= 90% of N; P3 era placement does not rescue cadence locally (R era < R hash at K=0.10); P4 shuffling the cadence assignment on the era map raises R(0.10) above the true assignment; P5 global coupling erases placement (R global >= both local cadence rooms at K=0.10); P6 at K=0.10 each era half is more coherent than the era whole and the era province gap exceeds the hash room's.
- **Status**: experiments/firefly-cadence
- **Evaluation**: P2-P6 HELD; P1 CRACKED by tie on the instrument. Headless on this clone's real history (200 commits; gaps min 0.003 h, median 1.839 h, max 1,631.1 h; cadence omega min 0.0600, mean 0.1310, max 0.1600): corpse R(K) 0.324/0.752/0.943/0.998/1.000, Kc 0.100, at K=0.10 clusters 1, largest 200, flashes 601, provinces 0.960/0.968, gap 0.420; cadence-hash 0.028/0.315/0.841/0.989/0.998, Kc 0.100, clusters 1, largest 199, flashes 597, provinces 0.908/0.881, gap 0.691; cadence-era 0.112/0.274/0.326/0.975/0.992, Kc 0.200, clusters 1, largest 195, flashes 606, provinces 0.609/0.487, gap 1.883; shuffled-era 0.153/0.123/0.723/0.990/0.998, Kc 0.200, clusters 1, largest 197, flashes 621, provinces 0.735/0.787, gap 0.637; cadence-global 0.070/0.183/0.975/0.994/0.999, Kc 0.100, clusters 1, largest 199, flashes 589, provinces 0.976/0.974, gap 0.008. P1's crack, kept plain: Kc ties at 0.100 (corpse = cadence-hash = cadence-global) on the five-point grid even though the corpse leads every sub-critical reading (K=0.05: 0.752 vs 0.315 best cadence) — the ordering is real, the grid cannot price it; a finer K grid near 0.06-0.09 is the named follow-up. P2's fragment counts at K=0.05: cadence-hash 8 clusters >= 3, cadence-era 7, corpse largest 199/200. 10/10 unit tests pass (burst burns faster than lull; oldest commit inherits the median gap; era/hash placements differ only in x; shuffled room is a permutation of cadence on the same map; two coupled fireflies converge — the corpse's own test, kept; uncoupled advance is exactly omega; the mean-field identity holds to 1e-9; determinism; constructed cluster census; on the synthetic history the corpse out-synchronises cadence-era at K=0.10). Falsification condition stands in the README: indistinguishable cadence/corpse ledgers, or a shuffled room matching the era room at every K, would compost it. Neither occurred — the shuffled room differs from the era room at every K.


### understudy-fading
- **Parents**: experiments/fading-moths (The Moth Keeper's half-life survey, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave) x experiments/understudy-shoved (claim queues and a message bus, no field)
- **Concept**: The fading-moths role performed on wholly different machinery: caution booked by source — earned (own burns, delivered burn messages) against taught (evidence packets, the parallel counter `taught_caution`; an acquittal spends taught first) — and one pool taxed to zero at each dawn after the first, in three new rooms running this stage's evidence room verbatim. Twelve twin rooms, same seed; the nine inherited queue rooms pinned exact, the pool bookkeeping inert there because no dawn in those rooms taxes.
- **Novel trait**: It separates a decay claim into its ledger and its rent. The principal's census (which pool is large at the boundary) and its prices (what expiring each pool costs) were measured in one medium and read as one finding; the queue stage has no travel, so re-learning costs a turn instead of a walk, and the two halves can be priced apart for the first time.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README): bands B1–B4 (solo precedent, witness < solo, chain events > 5x witness, placed learners >= chain learners); P1 both packet kinds and learners > 0 in each fading room; P2 night-1 identity with this stage's evidence room, asserted by test; P3 the dawn tax fires by source exactly, asserted by test; P4 fading-taught burns > this stage's evidence burns (the claim — principal held by one); P5 fading-earned burns > evidence burns (principal cracked — taxing earned cost nothing); P6 fading-both burns >= the larger single-tax room; P7 fading fed >= 50 in each room; P8 the census — expired taught > expired earned (principal 41 > 13).
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-fading
- **Evaluation**: B1–B4, P1, P2, P3, P7, P8 HELD; P4, P5, P6 CRACKED — and the cracks are the result. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights x 1500 ticks per room, same seed): evidence (this stage) fed 57, burns 16, checks 92, events 634, acquittals 156, learners 11; fading-taught fed 55, burns 13, checks 92, events 633 (315 irregular / 318 steady), learners 11, acquittals 128, expired 99 taught / 0 earned; fading-earned fed 59, burns 15, checks 91, events 631 (284/347), learners 11, acquittals 143, expired 0/19; fading-both fed 54, burns 14, checks 87, events 596 (311/285), learners 11, acquittals 124, expired 158/18. P8 held louder than the principal (census 99 vs 19, 5.2 to 1, against the cave's 41 vs 13) — the census is a property of flood-teaching, not of the cave. But every taxed room burned *below* the untaxed room (13/15/14 vs 16) with no clean ordering, and the channel never starved (596 events under the both-tax, against the cave's 943 -> 393 collapse): where re-learning costs a turn rather than a walk, cross-dawn caution carries no protection in either pool, and what the dawn removes the night's first turns restore. The principal's one-burn rent for taught carriage was the cave charging for distance. 24/24 tests (inherited rooms pinned exact; night-1 identity and by-source tax asserted; fading totals pinned). Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `experiments/fading-moths`.

### waggle-locus
- **Parents**: graveyard/waggle-dance (the dead hive: 443 LOC on macroquad — scouts, a dance floor, recruited observers, leaderless quorum sensing over food sites; executed for Ecosystem Maladaptation / Skeletal Implementation, its headless GUI panic unresolved in the grace period; its DanceInfo handed observers the site index directly, its dance angle marked dead_code and never read, and its dance duration set to 100 x quality where its own README promised duration proportional to distance) + crates/locus (the living donation: Vec2 geometry with no window attached — the corpse reached for macroquad's Vec2 and received a GUI as a side effect; locus is the same geometry display-free, which is precisely the organ a headless bypass is made of)
- **Concept**: The corpse's hive run three times on identical purpose-split SplitMix64 streams (scout / recruit / dance-noise / search, one seed for all channels), once per channel, so the dance itself is the only variable. 60 bees; sites A (distance 60, quality 0.45), B (115, 0.70), C (175, 0.95); dance length carries quality (10 + 26q ticks), waggle-run length carries distance (10 units per tick of run — the encoding the corpse's README promised and its code forgot). Label: the corpse's channel, identity and true position handed over. Vector: only angle + run are transmitted, decoded through noise (angle sigma 0.085 rad, run sigma 7%), recruits fly to the decoded point and search it (detection 12 units). Quality-only: quality advertised, no vector, recruits fly a random bearing to a random range and search. Quorum = first tick half the hive's last-foraged site is C. The corpse's post-dance coin (re-visit with probability = quality, else watch) is restored and named; the first build dropped it and locked onto the nearest site in every channel, recorded in the README.
- **Novel trait**: The first garden specimen to price a communication channel by its payload rather than its effect: three hives identical except in what a dance is allowed to carry, showing a label channel's perfect ledger (miss exactly 0.00 at every site) is the signature of a cheat rather than a dance, a true vector channel paying an itemised distance tax (mean miss 5.83 / 11.26 / 15.61 by site, far 2.68x near; 74 of 495 searches failed; quorum delayed 1.37x) and still landing unanimous, and a quality-only channel starving its own signalling (dances 313 vs 2,666) because blind search eats the hours dancing needs. No prior cast specimen separates what a signal says from what it costs to say it.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 label reaches quorum first; P2 vector reaches quorum at C within 3x label's tick; P3 quality-only reaches no quorum in 6,000 ticks; P4 vector mean miss at C exceeds 1.5x its miss at A; P5 vector's final committed plurality is C; P6 label mean miss is exactly 0.0 at every site.
- **Status**: experiments/waggle-locus
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD, P6 exactly. Label: quorum 761; forages 457/160/2,249; committed 0/0/60/0; recruits 454; dances 2,862; miss 0.00/0.00/0.00. Vector: quorum 1,043 (1.37x label); forages 446/174/2,065; committed 0/0/60/0; recruits 496; searches 495, successes 421 (85.0%); dances 2,666; miss 5.83/11.26/15.61. Quality-only: no quorum; forages 78/48/163; committed 24/3/12/21; recruits 1,441; searches 1,431, successes 55 (3.8%); dances 313; miss 132.11/160.99/199.59. 6/6 unit tests pass (noiseless decode recovers each site exactly; run carries distance while dance length carries quality; label never misses; decode miss grows with distance; determinism; vector beats blind search on miss). Tuning recorded in the README, not hidden: the pre-coin first build posted no quorum anywhere (label forages 6,882/126/43, committed 57/2/1 to the nearest site — cycle frequency beat quality), and searcher detection was set to 12 units from the first run's 55/55 search successes at 22. Falsification condition stands in the README: had quality-only matched vector's quorum, the vector would carry nothing; had miss not grown with distance, the channel would be a label in costume. Neither occurred.


### listener-twinwalled
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-drifting (succession — the principal's named two-walls seed, still half, grown) x experiments/listener-paired (the fixed-record audit the judging is priced on)
- **Concept**: The paired audit behind two walls at once. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per regime, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; per-contribution noise in [-sigma, +sigma] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity and the contribution's channel — own, near wall, far wall each carry their own draw; arrivals under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is recorded behind three pairs of walls, the near at rho 0.55 and the far at rho 0.40, the record window extended to offset 20: pair (3, 8) separated (near echoes interleaved at 3/7/11; far's first echo coincident with the third own glint at offset 8); pair (4, 9) (near coincident at 4/8/12 — echoed's wall alone; far interleaved late at 9/13/17); pair (4, 8) both coincident (offset 8 sums own_2 + 0.55·own_1 + 0.40·own_0 in one arrival; offset 12 sums two orphans; only five arrivals reach the ear). Three judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets only): naive (first three arrivals vs the 0.30 spread line), single (listener-adrift's search verbatim — one fixed delay in 1..=12 and rho in [0.00, 0.95] at 0.01 steps, each candidate deriving its implied own glints and scored by summed absolute prediction-vs-delivery difference over every offset either record mentions; never told there are two walls), twin (a joint search over an ordered pair of distinct delays in 1..=12 and two strengths on a 0.05 grid, the true strengths on that grid: each candidate derives its own glints — subtracting, in strike order, any earlier glint's echo from either wall landing exactly on a later own offset — predicts the whole record, and is scored the same way; the true pair and strengths are the book's ground truth, never given to the judge).
- **Novel trait**: The first Listener planting where the record has two authors. Every previous cave held at most one wall, so a judge's vocabulary of one address (adrift), one trajectory (drifting), or one assumed address (echoed) could in principle suffice; here the surviving judge must count to two and price four unknowns jointly, and the one-wall judge's failure is measured as the lineage's control.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) two walls wound the naive judge at every pair (sigma 0.00 naive errors >= 20 at each pair); (P2) the single search is a one-wall judge (sigma 0.00 single errors >= 20 at each pair); (P3) the twin search prices both walls in a clean cave (sigma 0.00: errors 0 at every pair, delay-pair exact on every train, rho error max <= 0.011); (P4) pooled at sigma 0.10 the twin judge errs less than the naive judge; (P5) at sigma 0.10 the twin judge's delay-pair estimate is exact on >= 90% of trains pooled; (P6) at sigma 0.30 pooled the twin judge still errs less than the naive judge.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-twinwalled
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD (P1-P6), with P6's per-pair crack kept. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per regime across 12 regimes (3 pairs x 4 sigmas); the unwalled control reproduces the lineage's clean book exactly (naive errors 0 / 21 / 62 / 98 at sigma 0.00 / 0.10 / 0.20 / 0.30). Sigma 0.00: naive 120 (pair (3, 8); wrong 0, fooled 120) / 140 (pair (4, 9); wrong 20, fooled 120 — echoed's ledger exact) / 120 (pair (4, 8); wrong 0, fooled 120); single 128 / 140 / 190 (at (4, 9) the naive ledger exactly — naming its one visible wall buys nothing; at (4, 8) worse than naive by 70, manufacturing 70 wrong refusals unwinding one wall out of two-wall trains); twin 0 at every pair, pair exact 240/240 each, rho error mean and max 0.000. Sigma 0.10: twin pooled 181 vs naive pooled 378, pair exact 699/720 pooled (233 / 238 / 228 by pair); twin by pair 34 / 51 / 96, rho error mean at the coincident pair 0.150 vs 0.050 at the separated pair. Sigma 0.20: twin 83 / 91 / 104 by pair, pair exact 186 / 194 / 192, rho error max 0.550 (strength estimates collapsing to the grid floor while addresses stay named). Sigma 0.30: twin pooled 343 vs naive 354 (pooled clean floor 294 — the thinnest pooled margin in the lineage), inverting per-pair at (4, 8) (twin 127 vs naive 115); at (3, 8) the twin and single ledgers coincide exactly (109, wrong 18, fooled 91, both — kept as measured). 10/10 unit tests pass (clean separation over every phase, both pairs' arrival geometry exact to 1e-12 including the triple sum at offset 8, every pair wounding naive and single at sigma 0 while the twin search recovers every pair on whole books and individual trains, the derive step unwinding a hand-built triple-summed record, pairing determinism, noise bounds and channel separation). Succession named in the README: two walls at once with either in motion (pair x drift); a wall that accelerates; a judge that must first count the walls (one, two, or none) before pricing any of them — the single judge's 190 stands as the price of guessing the count wrong by one.


### listener-drifting
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-adrift (succession — the principal's named drifting-delay seed, grown) x experiments/listener-paired (the fixed-record audit the judging is priced on)
- **Concept**: The paired audit behind a wall in motion. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per regime, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; per-contribution noise in [-sigma, +sigma] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity; arrivals under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is recorded behind three walls at rho 0.55, all starting at delay 4 (listener-echoed's address) and drifting -1, 0, or +1 ticks per glint, the record window extended to offset 20. Drift 0 is the still wall (echoes at 4/8/12); drift +1 walks away (echoes at 4/9/14 — the first echo coincident with the second own glint, the rest interleaved and orphaned); drift -1 walks back (echoes at 4/7/10). Three judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets only): naive (first three arrivals vs the 0.30 spread line), static (listener-adrift's search verbatim — one fixed delay in 1..=12 and rho in [0.00, 0.95] at 0.01 steps, each candidate deriving its implied own glints and scored by summed absolute prediction-vs-delivery difference over every offset either record mentions; never told the wall moves), drift (a joint search over base delay 2..=6, drift in {-1, 0, +1}, and rho on the same grid: each candidate derives its own glints — subtracting, in strike order, any earlier glint's echo landing exactly on a later own offset — predicts the whole record, and is scored the same way; the true base, drift, and rho are the book's ground truth, never given to the judge).
- **Novel trait**: The first Listener planting where the wall's address changes inside one train. Every previous wall stood still, so corruption could be priced once its single address was found; here the orphan sits at a different offset for every glint, and the surviving judge prices a trajectory (base, drift, rho together) instead of a place.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) the moving wall wounds the naive judge at every drift (sigma 0.00 naive errors >= 20 at each of drifts -1, 0, +1); (P2) the static search is a zero-velocity judge (sigma 0.00: errors 0 at drift 0, errors >= 20 at drifts -1 and +1); (P3) the drift search prices all three unknowns in a clean cave (sigma 0.00: errors 0 at every drift, (base, drift) exact on every train, rho error max <= 0.011); (P4) pooled at sigma 0.10 the drift judge errs less than the naive judge; (P5) at sigma 0.10 the drift judge's (base, drift) estimate is exact on >= 90% of trains pooled; (P6) at sigma 0.30 pooled the drift judge still errs less than the naive judge.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-drifting
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD (P1-P6). Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per regime across 12 regimes (3 drifts x 4 sigmas); the unwalled control reproduces the lineage's clean book exactly (naive errors 0 / 21 / 62 / 98 at sigma 0.00 / 0.10 / 0.20 / 0.30). Sigma 0.00: naive 120 (drift -1; wrong 0, fooled 120) / 140 (drift 0; wrong 20, fooled 120 — echoed's ledger exact) / 140 (drift +1; wrong 20, fooled 120); static 0 at drift 0 and 140 at both drifting walls (on the backward wall worse than naive's 120 — unwinding a still wall that is not there manufactures 20 wrong refusals); drift 0 at every drift, (base, drift) exact 240/240 each, rho error mean and max 0.000. Sigma 0.10: drift pooled 124 vs naive pooled 386, (base, drift) exact 720/720 pooled; by drift drift-errors 36 / 52 / 36 (at drift 0 identical to the static judge's 52 — the winning candidate stands still); rho error mean 0.071 at the drifting walls vs 0.115 at the still wall. Sigma 0.20: drift 79 / 90 / 79 by drift, (base, drift) exact 239 / 235 / 239, rho error max 0.550 (strength estimates collapsing to the grid floor while the motion is still named). Sigma 0.30: drift pooled 301 vs naive 360 (pooled clean floor 294), (base, drift) exact 230 / 210 / 230. Symmetry kept honestly: drifts -1 and +1 return identical static and drift ledgers at every sigma (mirror records — delays {4,3,2} vs {4,5,6}); only the naive judge, reading arrival order, separates them, and only at sigma 0.00 (120 vs 140). 13/13 unit tests pass (clean separation over every phase, the still wall reproducing echoed's naive ledger 140, both drifting walls' arrival geometry exact to 1e-12, the static search recovering only the still wall, the drift search recovering every drift on whole books and individual trains, the drift deconvolution unwinding a hand-built coincident record, naive wounded >= 20 at every drift, pairing determinism, noise bounds). Succession named in the README: two walls at once, either possibly in motion; a wall that accelerates (drift itself drifting); the learned-timing gate in a room whose wall will not sit still.


### listener-adrift
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-echoed (succession — the principal's named unknown-delay seed, grown) x experiments/listener-paired (the fixed-record audit the judging is priced on)
- **Concept**: The paired audit behind a wall of unknown address. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per regime, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; per-contribution noise in [-sigma, +sigma] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity; arrivals under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is recorded behind three walls at rho 0.55, delays 3, 4, and 8 ticks, the record window extended to offset 20. Delay 3 interleaves echoes at 3/7/11, just before each following own glint, with no arrival at offset 12; delay 4 is echoed's coincidence (echoes on the next own glint, orphan at 12); delay 8 coincides its first echo with the last own glint and orphans two echoes after the window (12, 16). Three judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets only): naive (first three arrivals vs the 0.30 spread line), legacy tail (listener-echoed's judge verbatim — assumes delay 4, searches rho from an assumed orphan at offset 12, unwinds recursively; never told the wall moved), search (a joint search over delay 1..=12 and rho in [0.00, 0.95] at 0.01 steps: each candidate derives its implied own glints — correcting own offsets for coincidence at candidate delays 4 and 8 — predicts the whole delivered record, and is scored by summed absolute prediction-vs-delivery difference over every offset either record mentions; the cheapest candidate's own glints are judged on the 0.30 line; the true delay and rho are the book's ground truth, never given to the judge).
- **Novel trait**: The first Listener planting where the wall's address itself must be heard. Every previous wall stood at a fixed, known delay, so its price tag's location was given; here the orphan may be interleaved, coincident, absent from its assumed offset, or beyond the window, and the surviving judge prices delay and strength together against the whole record instead of reading one late arrival.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) the wall wounds the naive judge at every address (sigma 0.00 naive errors >= 20 at each delay); (P2) the legacy tail is a one-address judge (sigma 0.00: errors 0 at delay 4, judged 0 at delay 3, errors >= 20 at delay 8); (P3) the search prices both unknowns in a clean cave (sigma 0.00: errors 0 at every delay, delay exact on every train, rho error max <= 0.011); (P4) pooled at sigma 0.10 the search errs less than the naive judge; (P5) at sigma 0.10 the search's delay estimate is exact on >= 90% of trains pooled; (P6) at sigma 0.30 pooled the search still errs less than the naive judge.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-adrift
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD (P1-P6). Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per regime across 12 regimes (3 walls x 4 sigmas); the unwalled control reproduces the lineage's clean book exactly (naive errors 0 / 21 / 62 / 98 at sigma 0.00 / 0.10 / 0.20 / 0.30). Sigma 0.00: naive 120 (delay 3; wrong 0, fooled 120) / 140 (delay 4; wrong 20, fooled 120 — echoed's ledger exact) / 140 (delay 8; wrong 20, fooled 120); legacy tail 0 on 240 judged at delay 4, judged 0 at delay 3, 120 at delay 8; search 0 at every delay, delay exact 240/240 each, rho error mean and max 0.000. Sigma 0.10: search pooled 109 vs naive pooled 391, delay exact 720/720 pooled; by wall search 21 (= the clean book exactly at delay 3) / 52 (= the legacy tail at its own address) / 36 (vs legacy 125). Sigma 0.20: search 62 / 90 / 91 by wall, delay exact 239 / 232 / 232. Sigma 0.30: search pooled 309 vs naive 359 (pooled clean floor 294), delay exact 229 / 199 / 196. 12/12 unit tests pass (clean separation over every phase, delay-3 interleave offsets with no arrival at 12, delay-8 coincidence + double orphan amplitudes exact, delay-4 reproducing echoed's naive ledger 140, the legacy tail recovering only its own address, the search recovering every wall and naming each train's delay at sigma 0, naive wounded >= 20 at every wall, pairing determinism, noise bounds). Succession named in the README: a wall whose delay drifts within a train (a velocity, not an address); two walls at once priced jointly; the learned-timing gate in a room whose wall has also moved.


### listener-echoed
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-jammed (succession — the principal's named on-the-own-offsets seed, grown) x experiments/listener-paired (the fixed-record audit the judging is priced on)
- **Concept**: The paired audit behind a back wall. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per regime, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; per-contribution noise in [−σ, +σ] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity; arrivals under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is recorded with a flat back wall behind every reflector: the wall's extra round trip is exactly one glint interval (4 ticks) and every return re-arrives at strength rho 0.55, so the echo of glint j sums into glint j+1 at offsets 0, 4, 8, and the last glint's echo arrives alone at offset 12 (the orphan). Four judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets only): naive (first three arrivals vs the 0.30 spread line), gated (arrivals at the own offsets only — jammed's timing gate), tail (a rho search on [0.00, 0.95] at 0.01 steps whose unwound train best predicts the orphan, then recursive unwinding own_j = d_j − rho·own_{j-1} and a clean judgment; the wall's true rho is the book's ground truth, never given to the judge), restrike (a second train recorded at glint spacing 5, where the wall's fixed 4-tick delay lands echoes at 4, 9, 14 — between the new own offsets 0, 5, 10 — judged gated on the new spacing).
- **Novel trait**: The first Listener corruption that arrives AS the judge's own evidence — no extra arrival inside the window, nothing to refuse, the sums themselves lying. Both pricing channels are new to the lineage: algebra (the orphan arrival, read late and alone, prices the wall and unwinds the train) and geometry (a second chirp whose spacing moves the coincidence off the own offsets).
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) timing cannot separate coincident arrivals (gated errors == naive errors at every sigma); (P2) the summed step fools stones wholesale (sigma 0.00 naive fooled >= 100 of 120); (P3) the orphan prices the wall exactly in a clean cave (sigma 0.00 tail errors 0, rho within one grid step on every train); (P4) the tail amplifies noise (sigma 0.10 tail errors > clean naive errors); (P5) the re-strike escapes the wall but not the line (sigma 0.00 restrike errors > 0, far below naive, fooled 0 — spacing aliasing); (P6) at sigma 0.30 every channel sits on the noise floor (tail and restrike within 10 of the clean book's naive errors).
- **Status**: experiments/listener-echoed
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per regime, the unwalled control reproducing the lineage's clean book exactly (naive errors 0 / 21 / 62 / 98 at sigma 0.00 / 0.10 / 0.20 / 0.30). Echoed: sigma 0.00 — naive 140 (wrong 20, fooled 120, every stone), gated 140 identical verdict for verdict, tail 0 with rho error 0.000 mean and max, restrike 20 (all wrong refusals, no stone fooled). Sigma 0.10 — naive/gated 135, tail 52, restrike 36. Sigma 0.20 — naive/gated 123, tail 89, restrike 78. Sigma 0.30 — naive/gated 118, tail 100, restrike 99 vs clean 98. 12/12 unit tests pass (clean separation over every phase, exact wall summation onto the next offset with the orphan bare at 12, every stone fooled at sigma 0, the peak-phase moth flattening, gate == naive on every echoed train at every sigma, tail recovering wall and book at sigma 0, re-strike moving echoes off the own offsets, pairing determinism, noise bounds). Succession named in the README: a wall of unknown delay as well as unknown rho; the tail judge given the re-strike's train as a second orphan; the learned-timing gate with a wall in the room.


### understudy-evidence
- **Parents**: experiments/evidence-moths (The Moth Keeper's five-room tuition lineage, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave) x experiments/understudy-placed (claim queues and a message bus, no field)
- **Concept**: The evidence-moths role performed on wholly different machinery: every completed check posts its actual sampled intervals as a bus packet (delivery 0.30, matching the principal's ~6 deliveries per check); recipients re-compute the CV themselves on the same 0.35 line — irregular earns caution, steady can acquit hearsay caution. On this stage the sample is the lamp's own clock, exact and unmixed, where the cave's witnesses re-judged a mixture.
- **Novel trait**: It isolates the acquittal channel. The principal's evidence room could both teach and un-teach; the queue stage removes the mixture from the evidence entirely, so any remaining failure of evidence to protect cannot be blamed on fidelity — it must live in what the evidence *does* to the witnesses.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README): bands B1–B4 (solo precedent, witness < solo, chain events > 5x witness, placed learners >= chain learners); P1 both packet kinds > 0; P2 evidence-first learners > 0; P3 acquittals > 0; P4 evidence burns < this stage's chain burns (the claim — principal held by one, 12 < 13); P5 evidence checks < chain checks; P6 evidence events > chain events.
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-evidence
- **Evaluation**: B1–B4, P1, P2, P3, P5, P6 HELD; P4 CRACKED, and the crack is the result. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights x 1500 ticks per room, same seed): solo fed 61, burns 20; witness fed 50, burns 13; chain fed 56, burns 5 (305 events, 17 learners); placed fed 58, burns 4 (360 events, 50 aversions); evidence fed 57, burns 16, checks 92, packets 634 (277 irregular, 357 steady), evidence-first learners 11, acquittals 156, alive 24/24, honest refusals 0. Mechanism: steady evidence is true of the honest lamp but says nothing about the decoy, and 156 acquittals kept returning unburned moths to the uncautious state, where the next decoy claim is a burn, not a check — the evidence room never settles (burns 8/4/4 vs chain 5/0/0). Evidence survives third hand at perfect fidelity and still does not protect; the cave's one-burn margin was the mixture blunting the acquittal's edge, not hiding a larger win. 11/11 tests. Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `experiments/evidence-moths`.


### listener-jammed
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-paired (succession — the principal's named paired-choir seed, grown) x experiments/listener-choir (the foreign return, first priced as a hunt)
- **Concept**: The paired audit extended to the choir. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per regime, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; per-glint noise in [−σ, +σ] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity; glints under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is re-recorded with a foreign reflector's glints interleaved: own glints at offsets 0, 4, 8, foreign glints at 2, 6, 10, inside the same 12-tick listening window, at doses 0–3 foreign glints per train. The foreign reflector's kind and phase are fixed by the train's identity alone, never by the train's own kind.
