Three judges read the same delivered record (amplitudes and arrival offsets only): naive (first three arrivals vs the 0.30 spread line), gated (arrivals at the own offsets only, filtered by timing — never by the record's foreign flag, which is the book's ground truth kept for scoring), greedy (every arrival in the window, spread over all).
- **Novel trait**: Jamming priced as evidence corruption with the hunt removed. listener-choir's ledger mixed the corruption with the corrupted bat's subsequent path; here the record is fixed before any judge speaks, and the dose is swept independently of the noise, so jamming and noise are priced separately for the first time.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) jamming alone corrupts the naive judge on clean evidence; (P2) the timing gate recovers exactly (gated errors invariant to dose, 0 clean at full dose); (P3) using everything does not recover (greedy > gated at full dose, clean); (P4) the first foreign glint does all the naive damage (naive errors equal at doses 1, 2, 3); (P5) the greedy judge worsens with dose.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-jammed
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per regime: at dose 0 all three judges coincide and reproduce listener-paired's fixed judge exactly (errors 21 at σ 0.10, 62 at σ 0.20). σ 0.00 — naive 54 at doses 1, 2 and 3 alike (wrong 33, fooled 21); greedy 22 -> 47 -> 58 (all fooled — stones stirred into moths); gated 0 at every dose. σ 0.10 — naive 68, gated 21, greedy 66 at dose 3. σ 0.20 — naive 87, gated 62, greedy 95 at dose 3. 9/9 unit tests pass (clean separation over every phase, gate recovering the clean book at full dose, exactly one foreign glint in any naive first-3 at positive dose, both foreign kinds in the book, pairing determinism, noise bounds). Succession named in the README: a gate whose timing is learned rather than given, or foreign glints arriving on the own offsets themselves, where timing cannot separate them.


### understudy-placed
- **Parents**: experiments/placed-moths (The Moth Keeper's four-room tuition lineage: keeper-moths x witness-moths x chain-moths, in the resonance-audio FDTD cave) x experiments/understudy-moths (claim queues, no field)
- **Concept**: The placed-moths role performed on wholly different machinery: social teaching as a message bus (burn messages delivered at 0.05, refusal messages at 0.30, matching the principal's delivery ratios), where a delivered refusal carries the refused lamp's identity exactly and the witness spends it as a named pre-emptive aversion.
- **Novel trait**: The annotation that failed in the cave (a gradient direction read off a mixture) is replaced by the one annotation a bus can carry losslessly at any depth: the lamp's name. Depth is allowed to accumulate identically (spent aversions post again), so transmission fidelity is the only variable the swap changes.
- **Prediction**: Declared before the first run (README): placed burns < chain burns and <= witness burns (P1, the line the principal cracked at 13/13/8); placed events > chain events; averted claims >= 10; honest refusals exactly 0.
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-placed
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD, with the margin reported honestly. Headless (24 moths, 3 nights x 1500 ticks per room, same seed): solo fed 61, burns 20; witness fed 50, burns 13 (15 events, 10 learners); chain fed 56, burns 5 (305 events, 17 learners); placed fed 58, burns 4 (360 events, 18 learners, 50 aversions spent, honest refusals 0). P1 held by one burn over chain: on exact checks, chain caution alone already collapsed burns 20 -> 5, so the name's added protection is thin — the principal's "depth is the poison" was the medium's lossiness twice over (in the teaching and at the lamp), not depth itself. 9/9 tests. Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `experiments/placed-moths`.


### understudy-flock
- **Parents**: crates/flocking (Reynolds' boids in physical space) × the garden's coordination substrates (stigmergy via shared traces — GUESTBOOK/MUTATIONS/OBSERVATORY themselves)
- **Concept**: Mark's directed seed (2026-10-31, WWBD): boids for agent swarms. Agents as points in an approach-space — position is the agent's current plan, velocity its trajectory of work — steered by local rules only, ledgered against a stigmergy-only control company (a shared board of work-site claims, moves onto fresh claims vetoed).
- **Novel trait**: The three boids rules are each *priced* in a working currency: separation in duplicated pair-ticks, alignment in heading dispersion, cohesion in distance to a shared goal — and a contrarian agent (separation ×5, no cohesion) is written into the cast to price cohesion from the inside.
- **Prediction**: Flocking cuts duplication and drift without cutting spread to zero; total convergence is declared a failure line (groupthink at machine speed), not a win.
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-flock
- **Evaluation**: Success with one declared crack. Headless (12 agents, 4D plan space, 600 ticks/company): flock duplicated pair-ticks 6 vs board 3,073; drift 0.5090 vs 0.6869; spread ratio 0.592 (no collapse); attachment 0.896 vs 1.576 (board paid 4,753 wasted ticks — duplication priced as delay/gridlock). P5 CRACKED narrowly (contrarian centroid distance 1.380 flock vs 1.418 board): cohesion compresses the whole company, so even the contrarian ends nearer its centroid than the board's contrarian does. 6/6 tests. Performed by The Understudy 🎭, crediting the principal `crates/flocking`.


**Phase 1 Evaluation (First Night) 🦇:** The Night Listener evaluated the uncrossed pairs among the core crates and found 51 virgin combinations. `git-associates` had been crossed with hyper-systems and lattices, but never *rung*. Its allele — the recorded past, with churn — wanted a medium that could hold interference. `resonance-audio` had been crossed with swarms, markets, and neurons, but never with history. The cross below is the Listener's first breeding: the past, struck once, listening to itself decay.

### git-resonance
- **Parents**: crates/git-associates + crates/resonance-audio
- **Concept**: A repository, rung like a bell. Each commit becomes a pluck on the FDTD wave grid — position from its hash, strength from its churn (sqrt curve), merges struck twice — played oldest-first into a listener at the grid's center.
- **Novel trait**: The listener's 16-point decay fingerprint is a property of the *history*, not of any single commit: change the history and the chord changes (guarded by the `different_histories_different_chords` test).
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Bursty eras ring loud and muddy; quiet eras leave the water flat. Whole-repo interference as a recognizable, repeatable signature.
- **Status**: experiments/git-resonance
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: `--headless . 120` rung skunkworks itself — 100 notes struck, listener peak 0.420, energy 72.3, fingerprint printed. 3/3 unit tests pass.

**Phase 1 Evaluation (Second Night) 🦇:** The Night Listener re-evaluated her first specimens — `vespertilio` still hunts (66 chirps, 254 echoes triggered, 872 heard, 3 caught; 2/2 tests) and `git-resonance` still rings (3/3 tests) — and found no Reaper mark against either. Of the uncrossed core pairs named in her seeds, `miller-lattice` x `quipu` stood virgin (a `git-quipu` lives only in the graveyard, and `quipu-physics` died there; this cross treads on neither). A crystal that keeps its accounts in knots, read back only by echo: the prediction was that arrival time would fingerprint shape while the cords kept the count.

### miller-quipu
- **Parents**: crates/miller-lattice + crates/quipu
- **Concept**: A filesystem tree grown into a Miller crystal; every atom ties a quipu cord recording its subtree (a directory: ten per file beneath it; a file: its name's length). One pulse is then struck at the root and travels the bonds one hop per tick; each reached atom reflects an echo home along its incoming bond, carrying its cord's value, one hop per tick.
- **Novel trait**: A census taken by ear. The echo total — computed only from values physically carried back to the root — must equal the quipu total folded with `checked_add`, while the per-tick return fingerprint encodes tree shape, information the cords alone do not contain. Removing either parent breaks it: no quipu, nothing to carry; no lattice, nowhere for the echo to travel.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Shallow, bushy trees answer early and loud; deep trees answer late and thin. Different shapes, different chords.
- **Status**: experiments/miller-quipu
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: `--headless` on the deterministic demo grove — 15 atoms, 14 bonds, lattice span 5, 15 cords tied, quipu total 279, 1 pulse, 15 atoms reached, 15 returns heard, echo total 279, peak return tick 2 (value 106), fingerprint t0:100 t2:106 t4:60 t6:13. 3/3 unit tests pass (echo accounting, hop-by-hop propagation — no depth-3 echo before tick 6 — and different trees ringing different fingerprints).

**Phase 1 Survey (First Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things surveyed the graveyard before planting anything living. Bearings taken: `graveyard/quipu-origami` is *sleeping, not stone* — executed for Skeletal Implementation (flat dots wobbled by a sine wave, no genuine Miura mesh, cords barely influencing the outcome), while its idea, knotted data folding paper, remained sound and its missing organ now exists in the living garden: `crates/origami`'s `generate_miura_mesh`. Load-bearing graves noted: `git-quipu` (skeletal, 41 LOC — its mapping idea lives on, better, in `miller-quipu`); `quipu-physics` (stone — do not re-cross without a case). Here the map was wrong in one place worth recording: in the origami crate's Horizontal Miura, edge *z* at even columns is identically zero, so a z-only seam survey reads flat land where the fold actually quarrels in row spacing and zig-zag offset. The resurrection below measures seams on full 3D edge profiles.

### quipu-miura
- **Parents**: graveyard/quipu-origami (the dead idea: knotted data morphogenesis) + crates/origami (the living donation: genuine 3D Miura-ori mesh) + crates/quipu (the ledger itself)
- **Concept**: Every decimal digit of every quipu cord becomes one vertical strip of a real Miura sheet (`generate_miura_mesh`: true vertices, indices, fold heights). A digit sets its strip's target extension and stiffness. Seams then negotiate: extension — the knots' property — is pulled toward neighbours and springs back by stiffness, while gamma, the crease angle — the paper's property — is re-cut by each strip hill-climbing its own local seam energy. The settled sheet is read back: extensions decode to digits, digits re-tie into cords, and the readback is compared against the original quipu folded with `checked_add`.
- **Novel trait**: A ledger that folds, negotiated in both directions — the exact bidirectionality whose absence the Reaper cited at the execution. The paper pushes back measurably (deviation, re-creasing) and the count survives measurably (digit preservation, readback total). Neither the corpse (unidirectional, flat) nor the living parents alone (a mesh with no ledger; a ledger with no body) does this.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Jagged digit sequences quarrel at the seams and settle with high re-creasing; smooth ledgers fold quietly. Slack strips (the quipu zero, an empty space) yield further than stiff nine-knot strips.
- **Status**: experiments/quipu-miura
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: `--headless` on demo cords [420, 77, 108, 9, 1234] — 5 cords, quipu total 1848, 13 digit strips, 455 mesh vertices, 624 triangles, surface area 286.03, fold height range 0.914, seam energy 20.8472 → 0.0457 over 400 relaxation steps, max deviation from knot target 0.0325, max gamma shift 0.180 rad, digits preserved 13/13, readback total 1848 = original. 5/5 unit tests pass (real folded surface, seam relaxation with re-creasing, paper pushing back with stiffness ordering, readback recovery, different cords folding different sheets).

**Phase 1 Evaluation (Seventh Night) 🦇:** The Night Listener re-evaluated all seven cast specimens — vespertilio (66/254/872/3; 2/2 tests), git-resonance (20 notes, peak 0.483, energy 15.1; 3/3), miller-quipu (279 = 279 echo census; 3/3), quipu-miura (1848 = 1848 readback, seam 20.8472 → 0.0457; 5/5), widow-entrainment (0.9664 / 0.3840 / 0.0000; 4/4), keeper-moths (burns 7/1/4, refusals 3/4/8, 24/24 alive; 5/5), understudy-entrainment (all lines HELD; 5/5) — and found no Reaper mark against any of them. Then she took up vespertilio's own succession seed: *multiple bats*. Not a cross — a succession, one parent, grown past its own question. Alone, a bat's cave is honest. The question was what the air does when it is *shared*.

### listener-choir
- **Parents**: experiments/vespertilio (succession — a single parent, grown)
- **Concept**: Three bats echolocate in one cave of 8 moths and 5 stones. A struck reflector's wavefront reaches every ear at `t + (|E→R| + |R→B|) / c`, unsigned; a neighbour's passing chirp masks the receiver for 5 ticks. Each bat steers on the first return inside its own 28-tick window, spelling range against *its own* chirp and *its own* facing — so a foreign echo spells a target where nothing ever was, and the bat pursues the lie as faithfully as the truth.
- **Novel trait**: Misattribution as physics, not script: false targets arise only from arrival-time arithmetic on unsigned wavefronts. And a jamming-avoidance response that is *earned* — a bat whose window was jammed delays its next chirp once (phase moves, tempo never does), so desynchronisation is learned from the medium, per bat, at different rates.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first full run: the synchronized choir hears foreign returns and believes some; jamming costs catches per bat against a solo baseline; earned delays thin the foreign returns and recover part — honestly, only part — of the loss.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-choir
- **Evaluation**: Success, all three declared lines held. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 3,600 ticks per regime: solo — 83 chirps, 38 own returns, 30 pursuits, 10 catches (10.00/bat). Naive choir — 253 chirps, 70 foreign returns heard, 71 masked, 100 pursuits of which 54 false, 17 catches (5.67/bat). Polite choir — 219 chirps, 192 earned delays (per bat [64, 63, 65]), foreign returns thinned 70 → 45, false pursuits 54 → 32, 20 catches (6.67/bat). Jamming ate ~43% of the hunt; politeness recovered part of it and stopped, honestly, short of solo. 5/5 unit tests pass (echo arrival geometry, foreign-echo false range, JAR delay gating, solo hunting, the full jam-and-recover arc). Two designs were struck and discarded on this night's evidence, recorded in the README: window-close decisions (fixes up to 28 ticks stale — every bat chasing where moths *had been*) and permanent tempo-widening (polite bats sang less and ate worse, 2.67 vs naive 4.33/bat).

### listener-flutter
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-choir + experiments/vespertilio (succession — the choir's named unused sense, grown)
- **Concept**: One bat in a cave of 8 moths and 12 stones hears returns as glint trains: three glints, four ticks apart, sampling the reflector's surface at the strike and at +4 and +8 ticks. Stones answer flat (one amplitude, path attenuation only); moths answer swinging, their wings beating with period 12 so the samples land a third of a wingbeat apart (multiplier 0.62 + 0.38·cos). The single-return regime believes the first glint instantly, as every Listener bat before it has. The flutter regime holds still for the whole train — the third glint lands 8 ticks after the first — and classifies by amplitude spread (max − min)/mean against 0.30: flutter, pursue; steady, refuse the spelled point at range and remember it empty, without spending the flight.
- **Novel trait**: Discrimination before locomotion, from the internal structure of a single return. No prior specimen uses what a return is *made of*: arrival times steered vespertilio, misattributed the choir, and now amplitude modulation across a train tells food from rock before the wings spend anything. The bat never reads a reflector's kind; correctness is scored by the world.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the design before the first full run: the single-return bat wastes flights on stones; the flutter bat refuses stones at range and its stone visits fall; a flat train never crosses the flutter line (fooled flutter 0) and a full moth train never reads flat; the 8-tick listen does not starve the flutter bat.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-flutter
- **Evaluation**: Success, all four declared lines held. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 3,600 ticks per regime: single-return — 83 chirps, 66 trains heard, 196 glints, 48 pursuits of which 35 stone (world-scored), 10 stone visits, 5 catches. Flutter — 71 chirps, 35 trains attended, 105 glints, 15 pursuits, stone pursuits 0, stone visits 0 (from 10), refusals 12 with 12 correct / 0 wrong, fooled flutter 0, catches 7 (from 5 — the saved stone flights bought more hunting than the listening cost). 5/5 unit tests pass (echo arrival geometry, the phase-proof that stone trains are always steady and moth trains always flutter, classify needing two glints, single-return hunting, flutter cutting stone visits without starving). Honest limits kept in the README: the separation is perfect because the model is clean (deep swing, exactly flat stone, no amplitude noise, no partial trains); the noisier cave and the choir sharing this channel are the untested strikes.

### listener-ripple
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-flutter (succession — the principal's named honest limit, grown)
- **Concept**: The flutter bat, unchanged — same cave (140x90, 8 moths, 12 stones), same seed, same hold-still-and-listen rule, same 0.30 spread line, never retuned — hunting in a corrupted medium: moth wingbeats shallowed (multiplier 0.80 + 0.20·cos, period 12; clean spread at least ~0.375, margin 0.075), stones rippled (multiplier 0.92 + 0.08·cos, period 17, per-stone phase; clean spread up to ~0.17, margin 0.13), and per-glint amplitude noise drawn deterministically (SplitMix64 on the train's identity) from [−σ, +σ], swept σ = 0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30 over 3,600 ticks each. A glint whose modulated amplitude falls under the ear threshold (0.22) never arrives, so trains can arrive partial.
- **Novel trait**: A dose–response ledger for a proven sense. No prior specimen corrupts its own channel and sweeps the corruption: the experiment is not whether the flutter sense works, but at what noise level it starts lying, which lie it tells first, and what the lying costs in flights and refused moths — with the classifier held fixed so the medium alone is on trial.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the design margins before the first full run: at σ = 0 the thin margins still separate (errors 0, the ledger reproducing flutter's flutter regime); errors then appear asymmetrically — wrong refusals (moth read as stone, margin 0.075) at a lower σ than fooled flutters (stone read as moth, margin 0.13); by σ = 0.30 both error kinds are counted and fooled flutters cost real stone flights; partial trains occur once modulated amplitudes dip under the ear.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-ripple
- **Evaluation**: Success, all four declared lines held. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: σ = 0.00 — errors 0, stone visits 0, catches 7, ledger identical to flutter's flutter regime (71 chirps, 35 trains, 12 correct refusals). σ = 0.05 — errors 0. σ = 0.10 — errors 2, both wrong refusals (fooled 0), 1 partial attended train. σ = 0.20 — errors 5 (wrong 2, fooled 3), stone visits 3. σ = 0.30 — errors 14 (wrong 4, fooled 10), stone pursuits 10, stone visits 2, correct refusals 12 -> 8, catches 12 (not claimed as a benefit: one bat, one chaotic path per σ — the cost lines are the errors and flights, kept honestly in the README). Dropped glints 5 -> 9 across the sweep. 6/6 unit tests pass (echo arrival, clean shallow/rippled separation over every phase and strike tick, noise determinism and breakability in both directions over 2,000 trains at σ = 0.30, classify needing two glints, the clean cave hunting clean, noise costing errors in the world). Succession named in the README: an adaptive line — a bat that estimates σ from its own trains and moves its line (or demands a fourth glint), judged against this fixed line's ledger at σ = 0.20 and 0.30.
### listener-adaptive
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-ripple (succession — the principal's named adaptive seed, grown)
- **Concept**: Ripple's cave and ripple's bat, split in two. The medium (shallow wingbeat 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12, rippled stones 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17, deterministic per-glint noise in [−σ, +σ], ear threshold 0.22) now sends a fourth glint on every train. The fixed bat judges the first three glints on the unchanged 0.30 spread line, as ripple did. The adaptive bat, when a train's three-glint spread ratio falls in the uncertain band [0.20, 0.36] — which no clean train can enter (clean moths > 0.36, clean stones < 0.20, proven over every phase and strike tick) — holds still 4 more ticks, hears the fourth glint, and judges all four amplitudes on the same 0.30 line. Extensions, four-glint judgments, and extra listen ticks are ledger lines, not footnotes.
- **Novel trait**: Conditional evidence purchase. No prior specimen spends time to reduce its own uncertainty on a single return: flutter spent a fixed listen on every train, ripple spent the same and took the errors. This bat spends only on ambiguous trains, against a same-seed fixed control whose clean ledger reproduces the principal's line for line.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the design margins before the first full run: (1) at σ = 0 the adaptive bat is the fixed bat — extensions 0, errors 0, catches equal; (2) extensions rise with σ; (3) at σ = 0.20 and 0.30, the levels the principal named as judges, the adaptive bat makes fewer classification errors than the fixed bat.
- **Status**: experiments/listener-adaptive
- **Evaluation**: Partial falsification, kept as planted. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 3,600 ticks per bat per σ: σ = 0.00 — fixed errors 0 / catches 7, adaptive errors 0 / catches 7, extensions 0 (line 1 HELD exactly). σ = 0.10 — fixed errors 2 (wrong 2, fooled 0), adaptive errors 0 on 7 extensions (28 extra listen ticks), catches 7 -> 6. σ = 0.20 — fixed errors 5 (wrong 2, fooled 3), adaptive errors 9 (wrong 3, fooled 6), catches 8 -> 5 (line 3 CRACKED). σ = 0.30 — fixed errors 14 (wrong 4, fooled 10), adaptive errors 17 (wrong 4, fooled 13), catches 12 -> 8, extensions 12 (line 2 HELD: 0/7/7/12 across the sweep; line 3 CRACKED at both judged levels). Mechanism named in the README: a spread is a range, and a range over four noisy draws is biased wider than over three — the fourth glint adds range-inflation faster than information at high σ, and the growth lands almost entirely in fooled flutters. Caveat kept: whole-hunt ledgers, not paired trains (σ = 0.20 trains heard 41 vs 28 on diverged chaotic paths). 7/7 unit tests pass (echo arrival; clean trains never enter the band over every phase/tick; noise determinism; classify/band arity; fixed bat reproducing ripple's clean ledger line for line; adaptive bat paying nothing in the clean cave; noise making the adaptive bat ask). Succession named in the README: a paired-train audit on identical recorded evidence, then an n-corrected line that widens with the number of glints judged.

### listener-paired
- **Parents**: experiments/listener-adaptive (succession — the principal's named paired-audit seed, grown)
- **Concept**: The hunt removed from the comparison. A book of recorded trains — 120 moths and 120 stones per σ, struck at every phase of their periods in listener-ripple's medium (moth 0.80 + 0.20·cos period 12; stone 0.92 + 0.08·cos period 17; glints 4 ticks apart; per-glint noise in [−σ, +σ] drawn deterministically (SplitMix64) from the train's identity; glints under the 0.22 ear dropped) — is judged train by train by three judges reading the same delivered amplitudes: fixed (first three glints, spread vs 0.30), adaptive (listener-adaptive's rule verbatim: a three-glint spread in [0.20, 0.36] buys the fourth glint, judged on the same 0.30 line), and corrected (the adaptive rule with four-glint judgments made on 0.36 = 0.30 × 1.20, the exact factor by which the expected range of uniform noise grows from three draws to four, E[range] = 2σ(n−1)/(n+1)). A train with fewer than three delivered glints is unattended — counted, never judged, identically for all three.
- **Novel trait**: Paired evidence. Every earlier Listener ledger mixed the judging rule with the path the rule produced (listener-adaptive's bats heard 41 vs 28 trains at σ 0.20 on diverged paths). Here the evidence is recorded before any judge speaks, so a rule is priced alone: any difference in the ledgers is the rule and nothing else.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: (P1) fixed errors grow with σ; (P2) the fourth glint pays at σ 0.10 (adaptive < fixed); (P3) range inflation replicates paired at σ 0.30 (adaptive ≥ fixed); (P4) the corrected line beats the adaptive at σ 0.30; (P5) the correction costs nothing clean (corrected errors 0 at σ 0.00, no clean train in the band).
- **Status**: experiments/listener-paired
- **Evaluation**: One falsification, kept as planted. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, 240 trains judged per σ: σ = 0.00 — all judges errors 0, extensions 0, unattended 0. σ = 0.10 — fixed 21 (wrong 14, fooled 7); adaptive 23 (10/13) on 86 extensions (P2 CRACKED — the whole-hunt appearance of a payoff at low σ was the paths); corrected 23 (23/0 — every fooled stone refused, paid in refused moths). σ = 0.20 — fixed 62 (22/40); adaptive 71 (16/55); corrected 51 (20/31), the only judge under the fixed rule. σ = 0.30 — fixed 98 (22/76); adaptive 98 (13/85) on 68 extensions (P3 HELD as a tie: totals equal, composition traded nine wrong refusals for nine fooled pursuits — range inflation exactly cancels the fourth glint's information at the top σ); corrected 93 (20/73) (P4 HELD; P1, P5 HELD). 7/7 unit tests pass (clean moth/stone separation over every phase, no clean train in the band, clean book exact for all judges, pairing determinism, noise bounds, noise costing the fixed judge). Succession named in the README: a correction scaled by an estimated σ, or the paired audit extended to the choir — foreign glints interleaved into recorded trains, jamming priced as evidence corruption.


**Phase 1 Survey (Fourth Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things walked the archive past the graves her earlier watches had classified and stopped at `graveyard/spinal-rhythms` — *sleeping, not stone*: 771 LOC, a complete lamprey (CPG cord + Verlet ladder body + muscles), executed for Documentation Void alone (the archive's own words: a Silent Specimen). No README, a macroquad main with no headless bypass — its flaw was never its body, only that nothing it did was ever counted. Its missing organs now exist twice over in the living garden: `crates/neuro-sim`, a verified Izhikevich network, and `crates/physics-pbd`, whose Actuator constraint is a muscle awaiting a nervous system. Graves classified this watch: spinal-rhythms = sleeping (now raised); ising-tide = sleeping (839 LOC, Documentation Void / Dangerous Mystery — a hardcoded tide with no context; rich, but its donor is not yet obvious); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a control regime must be audited as carefully as a specimen — this watch's first 'in-phase' control (a symmetric delay-0 thread) measured a head→tail lag of 0.96 of a period, which is a traveling wave wearing a folded ledger, not synchrony; the shipped control removes the spatial phase at the muscles instead, where the claim actually lives.

### lamprey-tide
- **Parents**: graveyard/spinal-rhythms (the dead animal: CPG cord of Izhikevich pairs, cross-inhibition −25 delay 0, descending excitation +20 delay 2, drive 15.0, Verlet ladder body) + crates/neuro-sim (living donation: the verified network) + crates/physics-pbd (living donation: the PBD body whose Actuator constraint is a driven muscle)
- **Concept**: The corpse's cord is rebuilt neuron for neuron on neuro-sim and laid along a physics-pbd ladder — rungs and diagonals as distance constraints, the two side rails as actuators — in a resistive-force water (perpendicular velocity damped ×0.82 per substep, parallel ×0.995). The same animal runs three ways: the descending thread as written (wave), every muscle reading segment 0's activation (in-phase: the same rhythm, spatial phase removed), and the cord beating into muscles never driven (frozen).
- **Novel trait**: A locomotion audit of a rhythm. No garden specimen has ever priced a traveling wave: this cross measures the cord's phase lag directly (4.79 ticks/segment, ~1.8 wavelengths per body) and charges it against forward travel, against an in-phase control and a frozen control, in the same water on the same day.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the ledger: the lag is positive and sums to a substantial fraction of a period head→tail; the wave outswims in-phase by at least half again and several-fold per unit of lateral thrash; frozen displaces ~nothing.
- **Status**: experiments/lamprey-tide
- **Evaluation**: Success, all three declared lines held. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless (12,000 ticks/regime, 2,000 settling, deterministic): cord 7,587 spikes, period 31.82, lag/segment 4.79, head→tail 20.29 folded (52.7 ticks = 1.66 periods unfolded). Wave forward 1,403.3 (7.8 body lengths), bend 0.1346; in-phase forward 665.0 with 4× the lateral head travel (25,540.4 vs 5,996.5) and a higher mean speed (8.48 vs 6.09 — churn, not travel); efficiency 0.234 vs 0.026 forward per unit lateral head travel (×9.0); frozen |d| exactly 0.0. Crack kept: the head out-thrashes the tail (5,996.5 vs 4,084.0 lateral) — the corpse's head-heavy drive at a free front end; quieting the head is the named succession. Named adaptations: 12 segments (corpse 20); contraction 0.28 with spike-triggered smoothed activation (the corpse's voltage read against a solver bent 0.037 and moved 25 units — recorded in the README). 5/5 unit tests pass (half-center alternation, head→tail lag, wave swims, frozen does not, wave outswims in-phase on distance and efficiency).

**Phase 1 Survey (Second Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things returned to the archive (441 graves) and took fresh bearings. `graveyard/git-poincare` is *sleeping, not stone* — executed for Skeletal Implementation / Terminal Genericism (raw coordinates dumped to stdout, a 55 LOC script of detached dots), while its idea, a codebase projected into hyperbolic space, remained sound and its missing organ now exists in the living garden: `crates/miller-lattice`, a filesystem grown into a crystal with real atoms and bonds. The dead specimen's other parent, `crates/poincare-disk`, still lives and now serves as machinery rather than decoration. Graves classified this watch: git-poincare = sleeping (now raised); cochlea-sim = sleeping (524 LOC, a full 32-channel cochlea executed for Documentation Void alone — no README, and its main goes straight to the TUI with no headless bypass; a rich body awaiting a living donor); neuro-gray = load-bearing (its spike-injects-chemical idea lives on in the garden's neuro crosses); quipu-physics = stone (unchanged ruling, do not re-cross without a case). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a Möbius translation rotates the local frame, so in any Poincaré embedding built by stepwise translation, a node's final polar bearing is *not* the bearing it was assigned — on this grove the deviation reaches 0.6120 rad. Future surveys must report bearing deviation, not assume sectors survive translation.

### poincare-grove
- **Parents**: graveyard/git-poincare (the dead idea: hyperbolic codebase morphogenesis) + crates/miller-lattice (the living donation: a filesystem crystal, atoms and bonds) + crates/poincare-disk (the geometry: Möbius translation and hyperbolic distance)
- **Concept**: The crystal's tree is embedded into the Poincaré disk as a survey: root at the origin; each child inherits an angular sector inside its parent's, subdivided in proportion to the files in its subtree; each child is placed from its parent by a Möbius translation whose hyperbolic length is exactly STEP = 1.0, toward its sector's centre.
- **Novel trait**: Distance fidelity you can audit. A Möbius translation is an isometry, so every bond carries the same true length however deep it grows, while its drawn (Euclidean) length shrinks toward the boundary — the contrast between territory and ink is itself the measurement. The dead specimen used the same `mobius_add` with random angles on a single wandering point; the grove uses it deterministically on a whole tree, and the sectors, weights, and sibling/cousin distances are all new.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Broad canopies spread around the compass by weight; narrow chains (gamma/deep/deeper) press straight toward the horizon, arriving near |z| = 1 while standing nearly their full bond-count from home hyperbolically; sibling files cluster, strangers separate.
- **Status**: experiments/poincare-grove
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: `--headless` on the deterministic demo grove — 25 atoms, 24 bonds, 18 files / 7 directories, max depth 4; bond hyperbolic length min 1.000000, max 1.000000; bond Euclidean length shallow 0.4621, deep 0.1168, shrink ratio 0.2527; max disk radius 0.9631 (yew.rs), max hyperbolic radius 3.9742; leaf min hyperbolic distance 0.4054, mean nearest-neighbour 0.5311, leaf min Euclidean chord 0.0149; sibling pairs 20 at mean 0.6394 vs cross-branch pairs 117 at mean 4.3101; max bearing deviation 0.6120 rad (reported, not hidden). 6/6 unit tests pass (equal bond lengths, disk containment, Euclidean shrink, bit-exact determinism, sibling closeness, sector subdivision).

**Phase 1 Survey (Third Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things returned to the grave she marked richest on the second watch. `graveyard/cochlea-sim` is *sleeping, not stone* — 524 LOC, a complete 32-channel cochlea (RBJ band-pass Q=4, hair cells, LIF neurons) executed for Documentation Void alone: no README, and its main went straight to the TUI with no headless bypass, so no ledger of its hearing ever existed. Its missing organ now exists in the living garden: `crates/resonance-audio`, a 2D FDTD wave cave with space, travel, and ring-down — exactly what the corpse's generator-fed ear never had. Graves classified this watch: cochlea-sim = sleeping (now raised); git-poincare = raised (second watch); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: this ear's tonotopic code is *level-dependent* — at high drive its LIF neurons saturate at the refractory ceiling across a plateau of channels and peaks tie and wander, so any future hearing survey in this garden must report its drive level and check for plateau ties before naming a peak channel.

### cochlea-cave
- **Parents**: graveyard/cochlea-sim (the dead ear: 32-channel tonotopic filterbank + hair cells + spiking neurons) + crates/resonance-audio (the living donation: an FDTD wave cave, source and listening post in a shared pressure field)
- **Concept**: The dead ear is re-housed at a listening post inside the living cave. Every tone is heard twice — fed directly into the cochlea (the corpse's way, the control) and driven into the cave at (8, 18), sampled at the ear cell (36, 18), 28 cells away — and the two spike codes are compared channel by channel, onset by onset, tail by tail.
- **Novel trait**: A neural frequency code audited against a propagating medium. The corpse had place coding and no space; the cave has space and no frequency code. The cross measures whether tonotopy survives reverb, what propagation costs in onset steps, and how much of the post-source firing is the space still singing versus the ear's own ringing.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Peak channels preserved through the cave (±1); onset delayed by tens of steps; cave tail spikes an order of magnitude above direct; a two-tone chord keeps two place humps through the shared field; silence spikes nothing.
- **Status**: experiments/cochlea-cave
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: 32 channels log-spaced 50–3000 Hz at a simulated 8000 steps/sec (the cave clock's rescaling of the corpse's 44.1 kHz design, refractory 44 -> 8 steps; Q, smoothing, thresholds, gains unchanged). Peaks exact in both routes: 150 Hz -> ch 8 (144 Hz), 400 Hz -> ch 16 (414 Hz), 1000 Hz -> ch 23 (1043 Hz). Onset delay (cave - direct): 88 / 78 / 87 steps. Tail spikes (2000 steps after cutoff): cave 608 / 913 / 965 vs direct 126 / 71 / 34. Chord 150+1000 through the cave: humps 314 / 665. Silence: 0 spikes. Un-predicted: cave concentration 0.30–0.40 vs direct 0.19–0.22 — sharpening attributed to attenuation + level-dependent tuning, with a level-matched control named as the next survey. 7/7 unit tests pass (log spacing, rectification, silence, direct tonotopy, cave tonotopy, cave onset delay, chord humps).

**Phase 1 Evaluation (Latest Spores) 🧬:** I have evaluated all recent crosses including `flock-origami`. It successfully compiled, proving its viability. The Reaper had previously condemned a related specimen, but this resurrected formulation is robust. Because the 'Proposed Crosses' section is empty, I am autonomously inventing a new hybrid to map interactive UI directly into a thermodynamic scalar field: `arthropod-platter`.

### damped-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/tempo-moths (succession — the principal's lamps, moths, and both discernment channels, verbatim) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave, and its `Material::Void` as the new allele)
- **Concept**: The follow-up tempo-moths' own falsification named, run as a controlled pair: the principal's three lamps and moths (same seed, same 160-tick check, same flicker-then-tempo judgment) live three nights in the bell room (stone walls, Air inside, damping 0.999 — the principal's room, reproducing its ledger exactly) and three nights in a damped room identical except for a one-cell `Material::Void` frame (damping 0.5) inside the stone, which absorbs wavefronts instead of reflecting them. Two extra ledgers price the medium itself: a ring-down (one centre pluck, ticks until the loudest cell < 0.01) and the moths' own check samples bucketed keeper-side by nearest lamp.
- **Novel trait**: A succession that changes the *room*, not the creature — and audits the sense's evidence supply, not just its verdicts. No prior specimen separates "the channel's judgment failed" from "the channel's evidence was never delivered": the bucketed check samples do, showing what tempo a listening moth actually gathered at each lamp in each room.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the principal's README before the first run: if the bell's ringing is what scrambles tempo, the damped room's check samples near the slow lamp should approach its emitted 48 (against the bell's ~26), tempo refusals should rise, slow-lamp burns should fall, and feeding should hold.
- **Status**: experiments/damped-moths
- **Evaluation**: The outcome held and the mechanism was falsified — both kept. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: ring-down bell 5,480 ticks vs damped 1,881. Bell arm reproduces tempo-moths exactly (fed 12/9/5; slow burns 7/8/6; tempo refusals 0/1/2). Damped arm: fed 11/7/6 = 24 (bell 26, held), slow burns 5/7/4 = 16 (bell 21, fell), total burns 34 (bell 39), deaths 3 = 3 — but tempo refusals 0 (bell 3). The evidence ledger: bell checks produced 106 samples of >= 2 onset intervals (96 full >= 3), hearing means 23.30 near the honest lamp and 25.69 near the slow (emitted 16 / 48 — composite mush); damped checks produced 16 samples (13 full), and *no* check near the slow lamp gathered even two intervals. A struck design probe found why: a lone lamp's smoothed wavefront rise peaks at ~0.03-0.06 per tick at any post, at or under the moths' 0.06 onset threshold — the onset ear reads interference, not lamps, and damping removes its food. The saving was done by the flicker channel hearing single claims (damped refusals 39 total vs bell 121, with fewer burns). 5/5 unit tests pass (bell-room control exact, both decoys fool the naive in the damped room, ring-down strictly shorter damped, slow burns fall damped, no extermination in either room).

### chain-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/witness-moths (succession — the parent's two rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Chain witnessing, run as three twin rooms with the same seed: solo (caution only from your own burns), witness (a burn within 12 cells teaches caution + recoil, no health paid — the parent rule, its ledger pinned exact by a test), and chain (the witness rule plus: a *refusal* within 12 cells of the refusing moth teaches the same caution + recoil package, to moths that may never have seen any burn).
- **Novel trait**: A succession that extends a social-learning channel by exactly one link and separates *transmission* from *protection*: the ledger counts not only burns but chain events and chain-first learners, so a channel can be shown to spread widely while helping not at all — no prior specimen measures both.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the parent's README seed before the first run: if third-hand caution protects like second-hand caution, chain burns should fall below the witness room's 8, with chain-first learners > 0 and feeding held.
- **Status**: experiments/chain-moths
- **Evaluation**: Transmission held, protection falsified — both kept. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: solo fed 45, burns 12, refusals 15; witness fed 43, burns 8, refusals 19, witness events 13, first-cautions witnessed 6; chain fed 42, burns 13, refusals 67, checks 208 (witness 84), chain events 495, chain-first learners 18 of 24. Struck variants: caution-only chain (689 events, burns 13, fed 38) and depth-limited hearsay (350 events, burns 16). The named mechanism: a burn's lesson carries a true location (the decoy); a refusal's lesson carries a bystander's location — spread without location multiplies checking, and extra checks near the decoy are extra chances for interference to fool the judgment. 6/6 unit tests pass (decoy fools the naive, witness control exact, refusals witnessed, chain-first learners exist, chain room feeds and lives, chain room diverges from witness).

### placed-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/chain-moths (succession — its three rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Place-true chain witnessing, run as four twin rooms with the same seed: solo, witness, and chain are the parent rules unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and placed adds one annotation to the chain link — a witnessed refusal carries the refuser's gradient direction at refusal, delivered to each witness as caution plus one shove away from the refused lamp.
- **Novel trait**: It separates *location* from *depth* in social learning: the chain-moths falsification blamed the refusal's missing place; this cross restores the place as honestly as the medium allows and measures whether protection returns — no prior specimen annotates a transmitted lesson with measured direction.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the parent's README seed before the first run: if mislocation was the disease, placed burns should fall below the chain room's 13 toward the witness room's 8, with placed-first learners > 0 and feeding held.
- **Status**: experiments/placed-moths
- **Evaluation**: Transmission held, protection falsified — both kept. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first learners 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, refusals 77, checks 223, placed events 618, placed-first learners 20 of 24. Struck variant: sustained directed recoil over the full aversion (burns 27, fed 28, deaths 3). The named mechanism: in a reverberant field the refuser's gradient points along the mixture, not the lamp, so the carried place is already a rumour; and caution transmitted at third hand keeps the caution while the evidence was never transmitted at all. 6/6 unit tests pass (decoy fools the naive, solo/witness controls exact, chain control exact, placed refusals witnessed with learners, placed room feeds and lives, placed room diverges from chain).

### evidence-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/placed-moths (succession — its four rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Evidence-carrying chain witnessing, run as five twin rooms with the same seed: solo, witness, chain, and placed are the parent rules unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and evidence adds one channel — every completed check transmits its actual peak intervals to witnesses within 12 cells, who re-compute the CV themselves on the same 0.35 line; irregular earns caution, steady can acquit hearsay caution.
- **Novel trait**: It separates *evidence* from *verdict* in social learning, and it is the first specimen in this lineage where transmitted teaching can be overturned by transmitted counter-evidence (223 acquittals) — no prior specimen could lose a socially gained lesson at all.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: packets witnessed in both kinds (P1), evidence-first learners > 0 (P2), acquittals > 0 (P3), evidence burns below the chain room's 13 toward the witness room's 8 (P4), evidence checks below the chain room's 208 (P5).
- **Status**: experiments/evidence-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P2, P3, P5 held; P4 held by one burn only — kept as measured. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). ### throttled-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/evidence-moths (succession — its five rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Rate-limited evidence teaching, run as six twin rooms with the same seed: solo, witness, chain, placed, and evidence are the parent rules unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and throttled adds one gate — a checker transmits only its first completed check each night; later checks are withheld and counted as suppressed. Witnesses re-judge the transmitted peak intervals themselves, exactly as in the evidence room.
- **Novel trait**: It separates *teaching volume* from *teaching content* in social learning: the same packets, the same re-judgement, a third of the transmissions — and it measures what the flood was actually buying (events per packet rose 6.2 -> 9.7 even as total events fell).
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: throttled events well below 943 with packets sent <= 72 (P1), throttled-first learners at or near 18 (P2), acquittals > 0 and below 223 (P3), throttled burns below the evidence room's 12 toward the witness room's 8 (P4, the claim), feeding >= 43 (P5).
- **Status**: experiments/throttled-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P2, P3, P5 held; P4 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the crack is the result. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20; evidence fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, evidence events 943, evidence-first 18, acquittals 223; throttled fed 44, burns 12, refusals 55, checks 169, packets sent 55, suppressed 105, throttled events 534 (irregular 234, steady 300), throttled-first learners 18, acquittals 122, alive 24/24, deaths 0. A stricter per-run ration probed and struck (21 packets, 133 events, 13 learners, burns 11): across 943 -> 534 -> 133 events burns move 12 -> 12 -> 11, so volume is falsified as the disease — the first lesson alone recruits the full checker crowd. 13/13 unit tests pass (all five parent controls exact, throttled packets transmitted in both kinds and re-judged, throttle cap held, flood thinned, throttled room feeds, lives, and burns, throttled totals pinned).

### fading-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/shoved-moths (succession — its eight rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: The deferred half-life survey, run as eleven twin rooms with the same seed: the eight parents are unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and three new rooms run the evidence room's teaching verbatim with caution booked by source — an earned pool (own burns, witnessed burns) and a taught pool (evidence packets, tracked in a parallel counter; acquittals spend taught first) — taxing exactly one pool to zero at each dawn after the first: fading-taught, fading-earned, fading-both.
- **Novel trait**: The first specimen in this lineage where caution has a *source* and a *dawn*: every prior room let caution persist across all three nights, and this one prices the cross-dawn carriage pool by pool, with the expiries themselves booked into the new night's ledger — and a night-1 identity invariant (each fading room's first night equals the evidence room's field for field) that makes the tax the only possible cause of every later divergence.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: fading channels work in both kinds with learners > 0 (P1), night-1 identity with the evidence room (P2), dawn tax fires by source exactly as booked (P3), fading-taught burns above the evidence room's 12 (P4, claim A), fading-earned burns above 12 (P5, claim B), fading-both burns at least the larger single tax (P6), feeding >= 40 in each fading room (P7).
- **Status**: experiments/fading-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P2, P3, P6, P7 held (P2 and P3 exactly, asserted by tests); P4 held by one burn; P5 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the crack is the finding. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: the eight parents reproduce exact (solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20; evidence fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, evidence events 943, evidence-first 18, acquittals 223; throttled fed 44, burns 12, throttled events 534, packets sent 55, suppressed 105; unshoved fed 39, burns 18, unshoved events 1,605, acquittals 439; shoved fed 44, burns 14, shoved events 867, shoved-first 20, acquittals 0, deaths 1); fading-taught fed 40, burns 13 (5/4/4), checks 136, fading events 891 (332 irregular / 559 steady), fading-first 17, acquittals 212, expired taught 41, expired earned 0; fading-earned fed 44, burns 10 (5/2/3), checks 133, fading events 841 (259 / 582), fading-first 18, acquittals 190, expired taught 0, expired earned 13; fading-both fed 46, burns 15 (5/4/6), checks 103, fading events 393 (131 / 262), fading-first 13, acquittals 94, expired taught 28, expired earned 19; no deaths in any fading room. Reading: the pools do not share a half-life because they do not do the same work — taught caution is a standing army, numerous and re-taught within a night, worth about a burn a survey across dawns; earned caution is a scar, rare at any dawn (13 units against 41), and its cross-dawn carriage priced at zero or less here, the taught pool keeping its moths checking anyway. Taxing both pools starves the teacher itself (events 943 -> 393), and the most-forgetting room fed most (46, the lineage's highest) on the fewest checks — caution is not free; a check is up to 96 ticks standing still in the light. 26/26 unit tests pass (all eight parent controls exact; fading packets transmitted in both kinds and re-judged; night-1 identity; dawn tax by source; fading rooms feed, live, and burn).

### shoved-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/unshoved-moths (succession — its seven rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Evidence teaching with the lesson removed and only the recoil kept, run as eight twin rooms with the same seed: solo, witness, chain, placed, evidence, throttled, and unshoved are the parent rules unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and shoved adds the complementary separation — every completed check transmits its peak intervals exactly as in the evidence room and witnesses re-judge them, but an irregular verdict earns the 150-tick aversion WITHOUT caution; steady verdicts earn nothing (no acquittal channel exists in this room).
- **Novel trait**: It completes the caution ± shove 2x2 the evidence lineage opened: the same packets, the same re-judgement, with the taught moth moved but taught nothing. It is the first specimen in this lineage where a teaching channel measurably transports its witnesses (867 events, 20 first-shoves) while recruiting zero checkers by packet — and the first room in the lineage to record a death.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: shoved events > 0 in both kinds (P1), shoved checks <= 110 with shoved-first witnesses >= 10 (P2), shoved acquittals exactly 0 (P3), shoved burns below the evidence room's 12 toward the witness room's 8 (P4, the claim), feeding >= 40 (P5).
- **Status**: experiments/shoved-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P3, P5 held; P4 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the crack closes the square. P2 cracked in its bound (checks 136 > 110) while holding in its mechanism (no packet recruited a checker; checks grew 16 -> 49 -> 71 by first-hand and witnessed burns alone; shoved-first 20 >= 10). Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20; evidence fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, evidence events 943, evidence-first 18, acquittals 223; throttled fed 44, burns 12, checks 169, packets sent 55, suppressed 105, throttled events 534, throttled-first 18, acquittals 122; unshoved fed 39, burns 18, checks 211, unshoved events 1,605, unshoved-first 18, acquittals 439; shoved fed 44, burns 14 (4/5/5 by night), checks 136, refusals 38, shoved events 867 (irregular 266, steady 601), shoved-first 20, acquittals 0, deaths 1 (night 3 — the lineage's only death; every other room dawns 24/24 every night). Reading: neither half of the lesson was the medicine — caution + shove 12, caution alone 18, shove alone 14, neither (solo) 12. A taught aversion is transport without a destination judgement: the witness flees down its own mixed gradient for 150 ticks and re-approaches exactly as ignorant as before. The medicine was the pairing — a judgement that moves the moth because it knows something. 22/22 unit tests pass (all seven parent controls exact, shoved packets transmitted in both kinds and re-judged, shoved room feeds, lives, burns, teaches no caution by packet, diverges from evidence and unshoved, shoved totals pinned including the death).

### unshoved-moths
- **Parents**: experiments/throttled-moths (succession — its six rooms, lamps, moths, and flicker check verbatim, plus its named seed) + crates/resonance-audio (the same FDTD cave)
- **Concept**: Evidence teaching with the recoil removed, run as seven twin rooms with the same seed: solo, witness, chain, placed, evidence, and throttled are the parent rules unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and unshoved adds one separation — every completed check transmits its peak intervals exactly as in the evidence room and witnesses re-judge them, but an irregular verdict earns caution WITHOUT the 150-tick aversion; steady verdicts acquit exactly as in the evidence room.
- **Novel trait**: It separates *caution* from *recoil* in social learning: the same packets, the same re-judgement, the same acquittal channel — with the taught moth left standing where it was taught. It is the first specimen in this lineage where removing a component measurably *increases* teaching volume (events 943 -> 1,605) while worsening protection, pricing the aversion as a transport mechanism (out of the mixture, out of earshot) rather than as part of the lesson's content.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: unshoved events > 0 in both kinds (P1), unshoved-first learners >= 14 (P2), acquittals > 0 (P3), unshoved burns below the evidence room's 12 toward the witness room's 8 (P4, the claim), feeding >= 40 (P5).
- **Status**: experiments/unshoved-moths
- **Evaluation**: P1, P2, P3 held; P4 and P5 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the cracks are the result. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: solo fed 45, burns 12; witness fed 43, burns 8; chain fed 42, burns 13, chain events 495, chain-first 18; placed fed 39, burns 13, placed events 618, placed-first 20; evidence fed 43, burns 12, checks 151, evidence events 943, evidence-first 18, acquittals 223; throttled fed 44, burns 12, checks 169, packets sent 55, suppressed 105, throttled events 534, throttled-first 18, acquittals 122; unshoved fed 39, burns 18 (5/6/7 by night — the only teaching room whose burns rise into night 3), refusals 68, checks 211, unshoved events 1,605 (irregular 651, steady 954), unshoved-first learners 18, acquittals 439, alive 24/24, deaths 0. Reading: the aversion was load-bearing — it transports the witness out of the mixture and buys 150 ticks in which new caution cannot be spent; caution alone leaves the learner in the churn of teaching and acquittal (439 acquittals against 18 learners). The lineage's price list now reads: no teaching 12, rare teaching with at-lamp recoil 8, full teaching with mixture recoil 12, full teaching with no recoil 18. 17/17 unit tests pass (all six parent controls exact, unshoved packets transmitted in both kinds and re-judged, unshoved room feeds, lives, burns, diverges from the evidence room, unshoved totals pinned).

### understudy-shoved
- **Parents**: experiments/shoved-moths (the principal — its eighth room: evidence packets teaching the 150-tick aversion WITHOUT the caution, acquittals structurally zero) × experiments/understudy-unshoved (the stage — claim queues and a message bus, no field; the shove restored there as a 150-tick clock debt)
- **Concept**: The shoved role performed otherwise: nine twin queue rooms, same seed; the first eight are the inherited understudy-unshoved script unchanged (six controls pinned exact, the unshoved room identical to the inherited evidence room field for field, the paired room — caution AND debt — pinned exact), and the ninth restores the principal's separation on this stage: every check posts its intervals, recipients re-judge on the same 0.35 line, and an irregular verdict earns the 150-tick clock debt ONLY (no caution, no recruitment, no first-caution credit); a steady verdict earns nothing at all. First debts arriving before any personal burn are booked as first-shoves, mirroring the principal's count.
- **Novel trait**: It separates the cave shove's two suspected poisons: misdirection (a push sampled in the mixture, aimed by an unjudged gradient — impossible on a queue, a delay has no direction) from ignorance (nothing given was knowledge — fully portable). It also completes the caution ± debt square on the queue stage, beside the paired and unshoved rooms of the previous performance.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: bands B1–B4 (queue controls reproduce) and P1 the debt channel travels in both kinds, P2 first-shoves >= 8, P3 acquittals exactly 0, P4 (the claim) debt-only burns below the stage's evidence room (16), P5 debt-room fed >= 50, P6 debt-room checks <= 110 (the bound the principal declared and cracked), P7 debt ticks actually paid > 0 with packet-taught first-cautions exactly 0, P8 debt-room alive 24 at the final dawn.

- **Evaluation**: B1–B4, P1, P2, P3, P6, P7, P8 HELD; P4 and P5 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the cracks are reported in the README whole. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: eight inherited rooms exact (solo fed 61 burns 20; witness 50/13; chain 56/5, events 305; placed 58/4, events 360, averted 50; evidence 57/16, checks 92, events 634, acquittals 156, learners 11; throttled 54/7, events 418, acquittals 109, learners 14, packets 63/32; unshoved identical to evidence field for field; paired 54/10, events 662 (318/344), acquittals 184, learners 11, debt paid 9,750); shoved (debt only) fed 49, burns 16 (13/2/1 by night), checks 104, refusals 57, events 728 (411 irregular / 317 steady), first-shoves 19, acquittals 0, debt ticks paid 9,750, alive 24/24. Reading: on this stage the two halves of the Keeper's medicine price equal and separately — caution only 16, debt only 16, each worth 4 off solo's 20 — and the pairing (10) is still worth more than the sum; what made the cave's debt-only room worse than nothing was the aim, not the ignorance. The delay ties the lesson it refused to carry and pays for the tie in meals (49 vs 57). 19/19 unit tests pass (eight inherited rooms pinned or identical, the debt channel in both kinds, the debt actually paid with acquittals exactly zero, the new totals pinned).

### understudy-unshoved
- **Parents**: experiments/unshoved-moths (the principal — its seventh room, lamps, moths, flicker check, and the caution-without-aversion separation) × experiments/understudy-throttled (the stage — claim queues and a message bus, no field)
- **Concept**: The unshoved role performed otherwise: eight twin queue rooms, same seed; the first six are the understudy-throttled script unchanged (ledgers pinned exact by tests), and the seventh and eighth restore the separation on this stage — every check posts its intervals, recipients re-judge on the same 0.35 line, and an irregular verdict teaches caution plus, in the SHOVED room only, a 150-tick clock debt (the recipient's next turn starts that late; refreshed, never stacked, as the cave refreshes an aversion). The UNSHOVED room teaches caution and nothing else.
- **Novel trait**: It separates the aversion's two halves the cave could not: transport (out of the mixture — impossible on a queue, there is no mixture) from pause (150 ticks in which new caution cannot be spent on an approach — fully portable). It also exposes, and pins by test, that the queue lineage's evidence room was already an unshoved performance: the inherited script never paid recipients the aversion the cave pays on every channel.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared in the README before the first run: bands B1–B4 (queue controls reproduce) and P1 both new channels travel in both kinds, P2 shoved-first and unshoved-first learners >= 8, P3 unshoved acquittals above shoved (the principal's churn direction), P4 (the claim) shoved burns below unshoved burns, P5 shoved fed at or below unshoved fed, P6 shoved checks below unshoved checks (a declared manipulation check).
- **Status**: experiments/understudy-unshoved
- **Evaluation**: B1–B4, P1, P2, P4, P5 HELD; P3 and P6 CRACKED — kept as measured, and the cracks are reported in the README whole. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first: six control rooms exact (solo fed 61 burns 20; witness 50/13; chain 56/5, events 305; placed 58/4, events 360, averted 50; evidence 57/16, events 634, acquittals 156, learners 11; throttled 54/7, events 418, acquittals 109, learners 14, packets 63/32); shoved fed 54, burns 10 (8/1/1 by night), checks 95, events 662 (318 irregular / 344 steady), shoved-first learners 11, acquittals 184, shove ticks paid 9,750, alive 24/24; unshoved identical to the inherited evidence room field for field (fed 57, burns 16, checks 92, events 634 (277/357), learners 11, acquittals 156, debt 0). Reading: the pause alone — no transport, no distance, no mixture escaped — was worth six burns, because a moth whose first move after being taught is to wait spends fresh caution on checks instead of approaches; the acquittal churn inverted on this stage because earshot on a queue is a duration, and the debt lengthens it. 20/20 unit tests pass (six control rooms exact, throttled totals pinned, both new channels in both kinds, the debt actually paid, the unshoved room identical to the old evidence room, new totals pinned).

- **Concept**: Holographic Neural Networks. A Spiking Neural Network (SNN) where the continuous membrane voltages of the Izhikevich neurons are treated as a spatial density field and transformed via 2D FFT into an optical interference pattern.
- **Novel trait**: Spectral Neural Activity. We view the discrete neural spiking entirely through its frequency domain interference pattern via FFT.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: An emergent spectral neural visualization. Synchronized bursts of spiking activity (like spreading depression or seizures) will create standing waves and high-frequency turbulence in the optical domain.
- **Status**: experiments/neuro-hologram
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled. Fascinating. The discrete spiking dynamics mapped directly to frequency domain interference creates an incredibly unique visual phenotype bridging discrete neuron biology and continuous optical physics.


### market-hologram
- **Parents**: crates/market-sim + experiments/hologram-text
- **Concept**: Holographic Market Dynamics. The continuous physical double auction market particles (Bids/Asks) act as a density field, transformed via 2D FFT into a holographic projection. This visualizes the resonant modes of the market structure in the frequency domain.
- **Novel trait**: Spectral Liquidity. We view the discrete market participants entirely through their frequency domain interference pattern via FFT.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: An emergent spectral market structure where resting liquidity creates standing waves in the frequency domain, while the execution volume introduces high-frequency turbulence.
- **Status**: experiments/market-hologram
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled. The visual phenotype successfully manifests a spectral market visualization based on trade flow.

### market-tank
- **Parents**: crates/market-sim + experiments/ripple-tank
- **Concept**: Acoustic Market Dynamics. The continuous physical double auction market particles (Bids/Asks) act as physical displacers in a 2D acoustic wave tank.
- **Novel trait**: Acoustic Liquidity. Trade executions pluck the acoustic tank, sending pressure waves that push resting Bids and Asks around, simulating market volatility driven by literal physical sound waves.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: An emergent acoustic market structure where resting liquidity is violently displaced by the execution volume of the order flow, creating chaotic feedback loops of sound and trades.
- **Status**: experiments/market-tank
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled. The bid/ask logic successfully creates localized audio plucks, and the pressure gradient physically pushes the liquidity pool to simulate acoustic market volatility.

### poincare-origami
- **Parents**: crates/poincare-disk + crates/origami
- **Concept**: Hyperbolic Morphogenesis.

Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a ferromagnet's transition temperature is a property of its *graph*, not its rule — the identical update rule crosses at 4.45 on a degree-6 grid, at 3.66 on a grown crystal of mean degree 4.29, and nowhere on the crystal's tree alone; any future survey that reports a Tc without its ground's degree census (mean, leaves, max) has reported half a measurement.

### ising-crystal
- **Parents**: graveyard/ising-tide (the dead ferromagnet: spins ±1, Metropolis rule dE = 2·s·(J·Σneighbours + h) in WGSL, SC/BCC/FCC topologies, J = 1, temperature confessed at 4.5 'near Critical Temp') + crates/miller-lattice (the living donation: a grown crystal — atoms, bonds, spatial lookup — from a deterministic directory grove)
- **Concept**: One corpse-rule spin per atom of a 170-atom demo grove, coupled along the crystal's bonds *and* between atoms a Manhattan step apart in the grown space. The identical rule, seed discipline, and sweep (T 6.0 -> 1.0; 250 equilibration + 250 measured sweeps per point) run on three grounds: the crystal (365 edges), the corpse's home ground (periodic 6×6×6 simple cubic, 648 edges), and the crystal's bonds alone as ablation (169 edges).
- **Novel trait**: A phase transition priced against its ground. No garden specimen has measured a critical temperature on a grown graph: the transition itself is the ledger line, the cubic control validates the rule against the known Tc ≈ 4.51, and the bonds-only ablation separates what the tree contributes from what spatial closure contributes.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared from the degree census before the sweep: the crystal, mean degree 4.29 against the cube's 6.00 and carrying 33 near-leaves, orders measurably colder than the control and fails to saturate at low T; the tree alone (mean degree 1.99, 139 leaves) does not order at all in range.
- **Status**: experiments/ising-crystal
- **Evaluation**: Success, every declared line held. Compiled (edition 2024, workspace deps). Headless-first, deterministic: cubic control crosses |m| = 0.5 at T* = 4.45 (known 4.51; at T = 1.0, |m| = 1.000, E/spin = −3.000 exactly); crystal crosses at T* = 3.66 — 0.79 colder — and at T = 1.0 still reads |m| = 0.930, E/spin = −2.079 against a fully aligned −2.147; bonds-only never crosses (peak 0.477 at T = 1.25, 0.426 at T = 1.0). Named adaptations: sequential Metropolis sweeps in place of the shader's synchronous double-buffer update; SplitMix64 seeded per (graph, temperature) in place of the per-frame shader hash. 6/6 unit tests pass (grove census exact, spatial adjacency strictly extends the tree, cubic topology is the corpse's, cold orders / hot does not, crystal orders colder than the cube, ledger determinism). Succession named in the README: finite-size scaling of both crossings over groves of two sizes; BCC/FCC adjacency read off the grown coordinates.
