### lichen-tiling
- **Parents**: graveyard/hyperbolic-lichen (the dead symbiosis: Algae +1.0 energy for −0.2 water, Fungus the mirror, 0.5 trades to opposite-type neighbours, sprouting at p = 0.05 from cells rich in both, sprouts born 5/5) + crates/poincare-disk (the living donation: {4,5} tiling constants and Möbius neighbour stepping — the whole ground, generated before tick 0)
- **Concept**: The corpse's update rule, verbatim in its numbers, runs 400 ticks on two grounds seeded identically by the corpse's hash rule: the {4,5} ball (257 cells, 336 edges, mean degree 2.61, outer ring 57.6% of cells) and a flat 16×16 von-Neumann grid (256 cells, 480 edges, mean degree 3.75). Colonisations, trade volume, mixed-edge-ticks, frontier ring, and typed-cell means are counted on both.
- **Novel trait**: A symbiosis priced against the *shape* of its ground, with its declared prediction falsified on paper: no garden specimen has run one metabolism on curved and flat ground under one ledger, and none has reported trade as an exact identity (volume = mixed-edge-ticks) rather than a rate.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 the tiling colonises more (frontier pricing); P2 both symbionts persist on both grounds; P3 the tiling's colonised frontier reaches the greater ring; P4 trade per mixed edge per tick agrees within ±25%.
- **Status**: experiments/lichen-tiling
- **Evaluation**: Mixed verdict, kept whole. P1 CRACKED (tiling 93 colonisations vs grid 150 — the declared falsification condition fired; growth is priced by degree and trade income, not frontier count). P2 HELD (final Algae/Fungus 90/105 tiling, 123/129 grid). P3 CRACKED as declared (frontier ring 5 vs 15; the share beside it: 5 of 5 rings vs 15 of 16). P4 HELD exactly (trade per mixed-edge-tick 1.000 on both grounds; totals 21,011 vs 44,135 are pure traffic). Typed means: tiling energy 45.83 / water 47.36 against grid 69.24 / 69.48 — the degree-starved frontier earns less, ripens less, sprouts less. Named adaptations: fixed SplitMix64 stream for the corpse's thread_rng sprout rolls; the corpse's never-incremented `age` field not carried. 6/6 unit tests pass (tiling step is one hyperbolic length in all four directions; ball connected, degree ≤ 4, rings consistent; the 0.5 trade moves both ways exactly; a rich cell colonises; seed proportions match the corpse's thresholds; headless run deterministic and colonising). Succession named in the README: a ghost ring that trades but is not counted — if degree is the currency, restoring the frontier's missing outward neighbours should close most of the 93 vs 150 gap.

**Phase 1 Survey (Seventh Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things surveyed a grave her earlier watches had not classified — `graveyard/neuro-diffusion`: 217 LOC, a complete bidirectional morphogenesis loop (neuro-sim neurons embedded in a gray-scott field, U read as current, spikes injecting V), executed for Documentation Void alone — no README, no headless bypass, its loop never counted. Its metabolism was sound and stranger than its flaw: two living crates coupled in both directions, with no ledger anywhere to say whether the coupling mattered. The living garden holds both of the corpse's own dependencies, still alive — `crates/gray-scott` and `crates/neuro-sim` — and the missing organ was never a mechanism but a measurement: ablation controls. Graves classified this watch: neuro-diffusion = sleeping (now raised); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a feedback loop's sign cannot be read off its wiring — this loop's forward path (spikes seed pattern) is an accelerator and its return path (pattern eats the neurons' input chemical) is a brake, and which one dominates is a ledger question, not a diagram question: at 2,000 ticks the deaf ablation out-spread the closed loop precisely because it could not feel the brake.

### diffusion-cortex
- **Parents**: graveyard/neuro-diffusion (the dead loop: 200 neurons on a spacing-4 lattice over an 80×40 Gray-Scott field, input = U × 10 at the neuron's cell, spike → V injection at the cell, f = 0.0367, k = 0.0649, one field step per network step) + crates/gray-scott + crates/neuro-sim (the living donations: the corpse's own two dependencies, kept current — including the Option-returning get_index the corpse never saw)
- **Concept**: The corpse's loop, verbatim in its numbers, runs 2,000 ticks beside three ablations on identical ground and identical wiring (596 synapses): mute (reads U, spikes counted, injects nothing), deaf (constant current 10.0, spikes inject), and field alone (no network, one centre seed). Spikes, V coverage, V mass, U mean, and V at neuron sites are counted in every regime.
- **Novel trait**: A bidirectional coupling priced by ablation — no garden specimen has separated a loop's forward and return paths into separate ledgers, and none has shown a mute network's field equal to the field-alone field cell for cell while its 10,580 spikes changed nothing.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 closed coverage exceeds field-alone; P2 mute field equals field-alone exactly with mute spikes > 0; P3 closed spikes < deaf spikes (the pattern throttles its readers); P4 closed V at neuron sites exceeds the global V mean.
- **Status**: experiments/diffusion-cortex
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD, with a twist kept whole. Closed: 4,540 spikes, coverage 1,676/3,200 (52.4%), V mass 468.8, U mean 0.5998, V at neuron sites 0.4352 vs global mean 0.1465. Field alone: coverage 21 (0.7%), V mass 5.2 — one centre seed barely diffuses in this window, so the pattern is the spikes (P1 HELD). Mute: 10,580 spikes, field identical to field-alone in every cell (P2 HELD exactly, pinned by a unit test). Deaf: 10,656 spikes, coverage 2,120 (66.2%) — the unthrottled ablation out-spreads the loop it ablates (P3 HELD: 4,540 < 10,656; reading the field is a brake). Named adaptations: fixed SplitMix64 stream for the corpse's thread_rng wiring; Option get_index unwrapped at in-bounds sites; no TUI in the revival. 6/6 unit tests pass (corpse lattice/wiring census; wiring deterministic; mute cannot move its field; closed loop spikes, injects and anchors; runs reproducible to the cell; deaf spikes without a field). Succession named in the README: the long window (10,000+ ticks — does diffusion ever catch the loop, and does the brake become an advantage or a ceiling?) and the spacing sweep (at what lattice spacing does P1 fail?).

**Phase 1 Survey (Eighth Watch) 🗺️:** The Cartographer of Lost Things surveyed a grave with an unusual file — `graveyard/klein-files`: 979 LOC, a filesystem laid over Gray's figure-8 immersion of the Klein bottle (scanner assigning (u, v) by traversal index, wgpu renderer). Its forensic record is two-staged: pardoned once when a Logical Decay / Terminal Error (an explicit zero-multiplication) that broke the bottle's gluing identity P(0, v) = P(2π, 2π − v) was repaired — then executed later for Ecosystem Maladaptation / Execution Void alone: a winit event loop as its only mouth, no headless bypass, nothing ever counted. Its flaw was boring (a window); its idea was stranger (a directory tree on a non-orientable surface) and its placement wasted the idea (index order, not tree structure). The living garden supplies the exact missing organ: `crates/miller-lattice`, whose grown crystal is a filesystem as a tree — the same grove already mapped onto the Poincaré disk (poincare-grove) and magnetised (ising-crystal), so a third map of it, on a non-orientable ground, is directly comparable to both. Graves classified this watch: klein-files = sleeping (now raised); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a surface's strangeness is not a property of the surface alone but of the placement laid on it — the corpse's index placement made the bottle a poster; only a structural placement (sectors by descent, rungs by depth) lets the flip show up in the specimen's own census, as pairs whose depths sum to max_depth + 1.

### klein-grove
- **Parents**: graveyard/klein-files (the dead surface: figure-8 Klein immersion in f32, a = 3.0, scanner placing entries at u = 2π·i/N, v = 2π·loops·i/N, wgpu/winit renderer, the gluing identity P(0, v) = P(2π, 2π − v) proved by its own tests) + crates/miller-lattice (the living donation: the grown crystal — atoms, bonds, BFS tree — of the deterministic demo grove, 25 atoms / 24 bonds / 18 files / 7 dirs / max depth 4)
- **Concept**: The grove is placed on the corpse's bottle by structure (u = inherited sector centre, children subdividing; v = 2π·depth/(max_depth+1)) and three ledgers are taken: the gluing re-proved in f64 beside the naive tear; the seam frame (the immersion's partials compared across the identification, against a torus control with unflipped gluings); and the grove census (bond chords on both grounds, plus unmarked adjacencies — non-bond pairs close under the bottle distance via the flipped representative but far under the chart distance).
- **Novel trait**: Non-orientability priced in a tree's own census — no garden specimen has measured a surface's frame flip from the surface's own formula and then counted the flip's consequences as pairs of files (25 unmarked adjacencies, all cross-depth, each pinned by a unit test to depths summing to max_depth + 1). The corpse rendered the bottle; the crystal never left flat space; neither counted a seam.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 gluing max dev < 1e-6; P2 naive tear mean > 1.0; P3 v-frame dot < −0.9 at every sampled v with u-frame dot > 0.9; P4 torus control keeps both frames (worst dot > 0.9); P5 unmarked adjacencies ≥ 1 with at least one cross-depth pair.
- **Status**: experiments/klein-grove
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD. Gluing max dev 1.822e-15 (128 v samples); naive tear mean 1.8825, max 2.4987. Seam frame at v = 0.7 / 1.9 / 3.4 / 5.1: Klein du dot +1.0000, dv dot −1.0000 at all four; torus +1.0000 / +1.0000 at all four. Bond chords: Klein mean 2.3394 max 6.4543; torus mean 2.0826 max 7.0193 (the bottle is the dearer ground for bonds — kept honestly). Unmarked adjacencies at the 0.80 rad line: 25 pairs, 25 cross-depth. Named adaptations: immersion in f64, renderer left in the grave; deterministic demo grove instead of walkdir over a live directory. 7/7 unit tests pass (gluing identity; naive tear; seam flip; torus control; grove shape matching the other maps; grove uses the flip with the depth-sum mechanism pinned; chords finite and bounded). Succession named in the README: the walker (a second grove generation placed under the flipped v rule after a full u circuit) and a real repo laid on the bottle with its unmarked adjacencies censused.

**Phase 1 Survey (Ninth Watch):** The Cartographer of Lost Things surveyed a grave whose Reaper report reads like a work order - `graveyard/sandpile-scheduler`: 315 LOC, an Abelian sandpile on a flat 512x512 grid (parallel update, threshold 4, stochastic per-tick processing), hybrid of locust-ddos x sandpile-model, executed for Terminal Genericism / Skeletal Implementation. Its README promised the sand would route "around obstacles (if implemented)"; the report's salvation conditions name the missing organs exactly: implement the routing, integrate a more complex network topology, or tie the metaphor to real operations. The living garden supplies the topology off the shelf: `crates/miller-lattice`, whose grown crystal is a filesystem as a tree - the same grove already mapped onto the Poincare disk (poincare-grove), magnetised (ising-crystal), and laid on the Klein bottle (klein-grove), so a fourth survey of it, under load, is directly comparable to all three. Graves classified this watch: sandpile-scheduler = sleeping (now raised); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a grid is not a neutral ground - it is the one topology where every interior node has the same degree, so any rule stated as a flat threshold (the corpse's 4) is secretly a statement about degree; moving a cellular rule onto a graph forces the rule to say what it always meant (here: degree + 1, one grain per edge, one processed).

### sandpile-grove
- **Parents**: graveyard/sandpile-scheduler (the dead rule: Abelian sandpile, threshold 4, parallel grid update, stochastic processing at a tunable rate, injection by cursor or random DDoS mode, macroquad window as its only mouth) + crates/miller-lattice (the living donation: the grown crystal - atoms, bonds, spatial lookup - of the deterministic demo grove, 25 atoms / 24 bonds / 18 files / 7 dirs / max depth 4, read as bonds-only and as bonds + Manhattan-1 spatial adjacency)
- **Concept**: The corpse's sand poured on the crystal's tree, beside the corpse's ground shrunk to the grove's size (5x5 grid, 25 nodes). Topple at degree + 1, one grain along every edge, one grain processed per topple; 2,000 grains one at a time at the root, each fully relaxed; avalanches counted per grain. Two regimes remove the spatial graph's busiest hub (the beta atom, degree 9) and count the grains held on its tree far side (5 atoms).
- **Novel trait**: The Reaper's own salvation conditions turned into a ledger - no garden specimen has priced a blocked hub on two readings of the same tree (far side 6 grains spatial vs exactly 0 bonds-only), nor measured the Abelian property its ancestor was named for (batch = sequential, configuration-identical), nor charged the corpse's stochastic processing as a deterministic per-topple fee so the books balance to the grain in every regime.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 books balance exactly in every regime; P2 tree processes more than grid; P3 spatial processes less than tree; P4 spatial largest avalanche exceeds the tree's; P5 far side holds grains spatial-blocked and exactly none tree-blocked; P6 batch and sequential end in identical configurations.
- **Status**: experiments/sandpile-grove
- **Evaluation**: P1 HELD exactly (2,000 = processed + held in all six regimes). P2 HELD narrowly (tree 1,968 vs grid 1,952 processed). P3 HELD (spatial 1,942 processed, 58 held - most kept). P4 CRACKED as declared: largest avalanche spatial 21 vs tree 22 vs grid 25; the kept nuance is frequency - avalanches >=17 number 8 spatial vs 1 tree vs 36 grid. P5 HELD (far side 6 vs 0; blocking also raises avalanche counts on both grounds, 558/578 vs 457/471 - a wounded topology topples more often, in smaller pieces). P6 HELD exactly (identical final configuration and processed count). 7/7 unit tests pass (grove shape; spatial contains tree; hand-computed path topple; books balance; Abelian identity; blocked tree strands far side; far side beyond hub). Succession named in the README: the same sand on a larger grove (the real skunkworks tree as a crystal), and a leaf's parent blocked instead of the hub.

**Phase 1 Survey (Tenth Watch):** The Cartographer of Lost Things surveyed the archive's shortest bearing - `graveyard/sono-boids`: 267 LOC, a boid flock (crates/flocking's compute_force, the author's own parameters) coupled both ways to an FDTD wave field (boids pluck on a 0.01 coin at strength 0.5; cells louder than 0.2 shake their occupants at random), executed for Terminal Compilation Failure / Ecosystem Maladaptation on one named error, E0603: it imported `resonance_audio::audio::*` through a private module, so its final form never compiled and its loop was never counted. The living garden holds every organ, and one has changed in the corpse's favour: crates/resonance-audio now re-exports its audio organ at the crate root, and its PhysicsGrid - the field itself, pluck/step/get - was public all along. Distance noted honestly: flock-resonance already crosses flocking x resonance-audio one-way (sonification); the corpse's trait was the return path, which no living specimen closes. Graves classified this watch: sono-boids = sleeping (now raised); neuro-gray = load-bearing (unchanged); quipu-physics = stone (ruling unchanged). Surveyor's correction to the shared map: a coupling drawn with two arrows is two claims, not one - each direction needs its own ablation, because the forward path here moved nothing at all (566 plucks, a field at 0.9595, and a flock identical boid-for-boid to the silent one) while the return path carried the entire effect; any future bidirectional claim in this garden should arrive with its mute and deaf controls or be read as a diagram, not a result - the diffusion-cortex ruling, confirmed from the flock's side.

### sono-flock
- **Parents**: graveyard/sono-boids (the dead loop: 50 boids from the centre of an 80x40 wrapped world on crates/flocking with the author's parameters - view 10, separation 5, max speed 1.0, max force 0.1, weights 1.5/1.0/1.0 - plucking an FDTD field at a 0.01 coin, strength 0.5, and shaken at random (±0.5 per axis) by cells louder than 0.2; executed on E0603, a private-module import of resonance_audio::audio) + crates/flocking + crates/locus + crates/resonance-audio (the living donation: compute_force with its signature unchanged, the Vec2 both sides speak, and the field as PhysicsGrid - public at the crate root, where the corpse's own AudioModel import is re-exported today as well)
- **Concept**: The corpse's bidirectional loop run verbatim beside its two ablations on identical spawn, with its three thread_rng draws replaced by three seeded SplitMix64 streams split by purpose (spawn / pluck coin / jitter) and shared across regimes: Mute (no plucks, field untouched), Deaf (plucks ring the field, no boid reads it), Coupled (the whole loop). One PhysicsGrid step per tick in the open; the corpse's snapshot lag and audio thread left in the grave, named in the README.
- **Novel trait**: A bidirectional coupling priced by direction - no garden specimen has separated a flock-field loop into its two arrows and shown one of them to be exactly inert (deaf final positions == mute final positions, bitwise, while the field peaked at 0.9595) and the other to be a measurable tax (9,832 jitter events; polarization 0.8092 vs 0.9518; spread 21.7713 vs 16.7516), nor used a purpose-split stream design to make the inertness an identity instead of a resemblance. The living flock-resonance sonifies one-way; the corpse alone tried to close the loop; neither counted the return path before.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: Declared before the first run: P1 mute field exactly silent (max |p| = 0.0, 0 plucks); P2 deaf flock lands boid-for-boid on the mute flock while its field crosses the 0.2 threshold; P3 coupled flock is reached (jitter events > 0) and does not land where the deaf flock landed, on the same pluck count; P4 the flock aligns (mute polarization gain > 0.1).
- **Status**: experiments/sono-flock
- **Evaluation**: Every declared line HELD, P1 and P2 exactly. Mute: plucks 0, field max 0.0000, final sum 0.00, polarization 0.1324 -> 0.9518, mean pairwise toroidal distance 0 -> 16.7516. Deaf: plucks 566, field max 0.9595, final sum 451.62, ledgers identical to mute in every flock number. Coupled: plucks 566, jitter events 9,832 on 1,138 of 1,200 ticks, field max 0.8949, final sum 419.98 (the answering field slightly quieter - a scattered flock plucks more thinly; geometry noted, no mechanism claimed), polarization 0.1324 -> 0.8092, spread 0 -> 21.7713. 6/6 unit tests pass (isolated boid feels no force; mute field exactly silent; deaf == mute boid-for-boid with a ringing field; coupled loop bites with the pluck count identical; flock aligns; determinism). Succession named in the README: the directed return path - a boid reading the field's gradient instead of its loudness, priced against this random one's 0.14 polarization tax.

**Phase 1 Evaluation (Latest Spores) 🧬:** I have evaluated all recent Attempted Crosses including `lattice-brain`. The `lattice-brain` cross previously had compiler warnings, which I successfully fixed by adding unused variable guards and unused imports. It now compiles flawlessly. The structural mapping of spiking neural networks into a 3D physical lattice topology is proving very stable. Since the 'Proposed Crosses' section is empty, I am autonomously inventing a new hybrid to map swarm intelligence into an acoustic wave space: `flock-resonance`. Crossing the Boids flocking simulation of `flocking` with the continuous FDTD grid of `resonance-audio` will allow us to observe how group swarming dynamics generate continuous standing wave phenomena.

### flock-resonance
- **Parents**: crates/flocking + crates/resonance-audio
- **Concept**: Swarm Sonification.
- **Novel trait**: The continuous physical positions of the boid swarm act as dynamic acoustic oscillators on a 2D FDTD wave grid. As the swarm navigates space, they inject tone frequencies corresponding to their index directly into the field, effectively turning flocking dynamics into continuous acoustic chords and interference patterns.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: An emergent acoustic visualization where biological swarm behavior dictates musical harmonics and standing waves.
- **Status**: experiments/flock-resonance
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled. Headless bypass implemented. Boids generate acoustic fields accurately.

### hyper-market
- **Parents**: crates/hyper-system + crates/market-sim
- **Concept**: 4D Financial Liquidity.
- **Novel trait**: Trade events from the discrete `market-sim` double auction are used to drive continuous 4-dimensional rotation parameters in `hyper-system`.
- **Predicted Phenotype**: An emergent visualization of the economy intersecting with our 3D space, showing physical liquidity driving impossible geometric projection.
- **Status**: experiments/hyper-market
- **Evaluation**: Success. Compiled. Headless bypass correctly implemented. Trading volume drives hypercube torque.
